1→# Centralize Postflight Pipeline
     2→
     3→## Summary
     4→
     5→Create a single engine-level postflight pipeline for the shared "real queue drained -> inject follow-up work" sequence, and have both scan reconciliation and review-import sync call it.
     6→
     7→This design works with these constraints:
     8→
     9→- The pipeline owns triage, otherwise the import path can still lose `workflow::create-plan`.
    10→- `target_strict` is always passed explicitly from callers.
    11→- `plan_start_scores` and postflight-scan completion stay in the scan command, not in engine pipeline code.
    12→- Lifecycle unification on `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase` is an intentional behavior change and must be tested as such.
    13→
    14→## Implementation Changes
    15→

    19→
    20→Behavior:
    21→
    22→- Default stays `True` so direct engine tests and existing callers keep current semantics.
    23→- When `inject_triage=False`, the function still:
    24→  - prunes stale review IDs from plan containers
    25→  - appends new review IDs to `queue_order`
    26→  - returns `ReviewImportSyncResult`
    27→- But it does not call `sync_triage_needed(...)`, and returns:
    28→  - `triage_injected=False`
    29→  - `triage_injected_ids=[]`
    30→  - `triage_deferred=False`
    31→
    32→This is the prerequisite that lets the new pipeline preserve `create-plan before triage`.
    33→

Perfect! Now I have enough information to generate a comprehensive report. Let me create my analysis:

## Comprehensive Postflight/Post-Scan Pipeline Analysis

Based on my thorough examination of the codebase, here's the complete postflight/post-scan pipeline:

---

### **1. POST-SCAN PIPELINE** (`desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`)

**Entry point:** `reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)` called from `ScanMergeResult` in scan workflow

#### **Phase 1: Force-Rescan Initialization** (gate: `force_rescan` parameter)
- **`_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan)`**
  - Clears ALL cycle state: synthetic items, plan_start_scores, scan_count_at_plan_start
  - Removes triage metadata (triage_recommended, etc.)
  - Prints yellow message if synthetic items removed
  - **Gate:** Only runs when `force_rescan=True`

#### **Phase 2: Issue Reconciliation** (pre-policy)
- **`_sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan, state)`** → calls `reconcile_plan_after_scan()`
  - **Only runs if plan has user content** (`queue_order`, `overrides`, `clusters`, or `skipped`)
  - Handles issue churn:
    - Supersedes dead issue references (no longer open/alive)
    - Prunes old superseded entries (>90 days TTL)
    - Completes empty manual clusters
    - Reconciles epic cluster membership
    - Resurfaces stale temporary skips (deferred→open)
    - Syncs skipped issue statuses (deferred/triaged_out)
  - Logs reconciliation with change counts
  - **Returns:** `ReconcileResult` with change count

#### **Phase 3: Policy Context Resolution** (depends on Phase 2)
- **`_subjective_policy_context(runtime, plan)`**
  - Computes `target_strict` from config
  - Computes `SubjectiveVisibility` policy (plan-aware, post-reconciliation)
  - Determines `cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`
  - **Returns:** `(target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed)` tuple

#### **Phase 4: Policy-Gated Syncs** (run sequentially with mid-cycle guard)

All of these are called within `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()` and guarded by `_is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state)`:

1. **`_sync_subjective_and_log(plan, state, policy, cycle_just_completed)`**
   - Calls `sync_subjective_dimensions()` with policy context
   - Unscored/stale/under-target dimension sync with objective backlog awareness
   - Prints: # resurfaced, # pruned, # injected
   - Logs: `"sync_subjective"` with `{"changes": True}` only if changed
   - **Gate:** Always runs

2. **`_sync_auto_clusters_and_log(plan, state, target_strict, policy)`**
   - Regenerates automatic task clusters post-merge
   - **Gate:** SKIPPED if mid-cycle OR force-rescan
   - Prints: "mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration" if deferred
   - Logs: `"auto_cluster"` with `{"changes": True}` only if changed

3. **`_sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Injects `workflow::communicate-score` when:
     - All initial reviews complete (no unscored) OR just imported trusted scores
     - Not already in queue
     - Score not already communicated this cycle (`previous_plan_start_scores` absent) unless trusted import
   - Rebaselines `plan_start_scores` to current scores when injected
   - Logs: `"sync_communicate_score"` with `{"injected": True}` only if changed
   - **Gate:** Always runs

4. **`_sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Injects `workflow::create-plan` when:
     - No unscored dimensions remain
     - Objective backlog exists
     - Not already in queue
     - No triage stages pending
   - Prints: "reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued" if injected
   - Logs: `"sync_create_plan"` with `{"injected": True}` only if changed
   - **Gate:** Always runs

5. **`_sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Calls `sync_triage_needed()` with policy (enables mid-cycle defer guard)
   - Injects triage stage IDs when review issues change since last triage
   - **Deferred result:** Prints "review work items changed — triage recommended" (dim), returns False early
   - **Injected result:** Prints "planning mode needed — review work items changed since last triage" (cyan)
   - Logs: `"sync_triage"` with `{"injected": True}` only if not deferred
   - **Gate:** Always runs (but may be deferred)

6. **Plan-start Score Sync** (gate: `NOT force_rescan`)
   - **`_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state)`** calls:
     - `_seed_plan_start_scores()` if empty or reset sentinel
       - Captures strict/overall/objective/verified scores
       - Clears previous_plan_start_scores sentinel (cycle start)
       - Records scan_count_at_plan_start
       - Logs: `"seed_start_scores"`
     - `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` if scores exist and queue drains
       - **Guards:** Don't clear while communicate-score pending (was just injected)
       - Clears scores once queue fully drained
       - Logs: `"clear_start_scores"`

7. **Postflight Scan Completion** (gate: `NOT force_rescan`)
   - **`_sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state)`** → `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready()`
     - **Guards:**
       - NOT if deferred disposition work remains (`build_deferred_disposition_item()` is not None)
       - NOT if objective backlog remains (checked via `_has_objective_cycle()`)
     - Sets `refresh_state["postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count"] = scan_count`
     - Logs: `"complete_postflight_scan"` with `{"scan_count": int}`

8. **Lifecycle Phase Sync**
   - **`_sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state)`**
     - Resolves phase from `queue_context().snapshot.phase` (work queue computation)
     - Normalizes to coarse phase name: SCAN, REVIEW, WORKFLOW, TRIAGE, EXECUTE
     - Logs: `"sync_lifecycle_phase"` with `{"phase": phase_name}` if changed
     - **Gate:** Always runs

#### **Phase 5: Persistence**
- **`save_plan(plan, plan_path)`** only if dirty flag is set
- Catches PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS and logs warning without re-raising

---

### **2. POST-IMPORT PIPELINE** (`desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`)

**Entry point:** `sync_plan_after_import(state, diff, assessment_mode, request=...)`
Called from `_persist_import_state()` in review/importing/cmd.py after state is saved

#### **Phase 1: Setup & Precondition Checks**
- **Load plan:** `load_plan()` from `plan_path_for_state()` or skip if no living plan
- **Compute policy:** `compute_subjective_visibility(state, target_strict, plan)`
- **Compute current scores:** For rebaselining (`ScoreSnapshot` from state)
- **Determine trust mode:** `trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}`
- **Exit early if no changes needed**

#### **Phase 2: Workflow Gate Syncs** (3 parallel calls)

1. **`sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy, scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import, current_scores)`**
   - Re-baselines scores immediately if trusted import
   - Sets `previous_plan_start_scores` sentinel
   - Records in `mutations.workflow_injected_ids`
   - Logs appended later via `_append_workflow_log_entries()`

2. **`sync_import_scores_needed(plan, state, assessment_mode, import_file, import_payload)`**
   - Injects `workflow::import-scores` ONLY if `assessment_mode == "issues_only"`
   - Checks stale pending import metadata
   - Records pending meta in `refresh_state["pending_import_scores"]`
   - Logs appended later

3. **`sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Same gate logic as post-scan version
   - Logs appended later

#### **Phase 3: Review Delta Sync**
- **`_sync_review_delta(mutations, plan, state, policy, sync_inputs)`**
  - **Gate:** Only runs if `sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta` (new/reopened/auto_resolved > 0)
  - Calls **`sync_plan_after_review_import(plan, state, policy)`** (pure engine function):
    - Prunes stale review IDs from queue (auto-resolved issues)
    - Adds new review issue IDs to `queue_order`
    - Calls `sync_triage_needed()` to inject triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard)
    - **Returns:** `ReviewImportSyncResult` with:
      - `new_ids`: issues added to queue
      - `added_to_queue`: subset actually queued
      - `stale_pruned_from_queue`: auto-resolved IDs removed
      - `triage_injected`: whether stages were injected
      - `triage_deferred`: whether triage was deferred (backlog pending)
      - `triage_injected_ids`: actual triage stage IDs added
  - Sets `mutations.import_result` and marks dirty
  - **Prints** via `_print_review_import_sync()`:
    - New review items (up to 10 with `short_issue_id`, "... and N more")
    - Stale review prunes
    - Footer with next-step instructions (plan queue, backlog, `desloppify next`, triage commands)

#### **Phase 4: Subjective Dimension Rerun Sync**
- **`_sync_subjective_queue_after_import(mutations, plan, state, policy, target_strict, sync_inputs)`**
  - **Gate:** Only runs if `has_review_issue_delta` OR assessment_keys present
  - Calls `sync_subjective_dimensions()` (same as post-scan)
    - Uses `cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`
  - **Then immediately auto-clusters** via `auto_cluster_issues(plan, state, target_strict, policy)`
  - Sets `mutations.stale_sync_result` and `mutations.auto_cluster_changes`
  - Marks dirty if either changed

#### **Phase 5: Lifecycle Phase Sync**
- **`_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan, state, policy)`**
  - **Gate:** Only if mutations are dirty (i.e., something changed)
  - Computes full lifecycle context:
    - `has_initial_reviews = bool(policy.unscored_ids)`
    - `has_objective_backlog = bool(policy.has_objective_backlog)`
    - `has_postflight_review`: checks for triage findings + assessment requests in state OR stale/under-target in policy
    - `has_postflight_workflow`: checks for import-scores, communicate-score, score-checkpoint, create-plan in queue
    - `has_triage`: checks for triage stage IDs in queue
    - `has_deferred`: checks for temporary skip entries in plan.skipped
  - Calls `sync_lifecycle_phase()` to resolve and persist

#### **Phase 6: Logging**
- **`_append_workflow_log_entries()`** — logs three workflow syncs
  - `sync_communicate_score`: `{"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True}`
  - `sync_import_scores`: `{"trigger": "review_import", "injected": bool, "pruned": list}`
  - `sync_create_plan`: `{"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True}`

- **`_append_review_import_sync_log()`** — comprehensive import log entry with:
  - `trigger: "review_import"`
  - `new_ids`: list of new review IDs
  - `added_to_queue`: subset queued
  - `workflow_injected_ids`: list of workflow IDs injected
  - `triage_injected` / `triage_injected_ids` / `triage_deferred`
  - `diff_new`, `diff_reopened`, `diff_auto_resolved`
  - `stale_pruned_from_queue`
  - `covered_subjective`: assessment keys covered
  - `stale_sync_injected` / `stale_sync_pruned`
  - `auto_cluster_changes`

#### **Phase 7: Persistence & Display**
- **Save plan** only if mutations.dirty
- **Print summary** via `_print_review_import_sync()` if import_result is not None
- **Print workflow injected message** if workflow IDs were added

---

### **3. RECONCILE REVIEW IMPORT** (engine layer)

**File:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py`

**Pure engine function:** `sync_plan_after_review_import(plan, state, policy=None)` → `ReviewImportSyncResult | None`

**Logic:**
1. Prunes stale review IDs (auto-resolved)
2. Determines IDs to add:
   - If triage baseline exists: only NEW IDs (post-triage)
   - If no baseline yet: ALL open review IDs (first import cannot drop work)
3. Appends new IDs to `queue_order`
4. Injects triage stages via `sync_triage_needed()` if work changed
5. Returns None if no changes, else `ReviewImportSyncResult`

---

### **4. EXISTING SYNC/ PACKAGE** (`desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/`)

**Current modules:**

| Module | Purpose |
|--------|---------|
| `context.py` | `is_mid_cycle()` and `has_objective_backlog()` — shared predicates |
| `triage.py` | `sync_triage_needed()` — inject/prune triage stages, mid-cycle defer guard, escalation |
| `dimensions.py` | `sync_subjective_dimensions()` — unified unscored/stale/under-target sync with objective backlog awareness |
| `workflow_gates.py` | Re-exports from workflow.py (app-facing stable API) |
| `workflow.py` | `sync_communicate_score_needed()`, `sync_create_plan_needed()`, `sync_import_scores_needed()` |
| `defer_policy.py` | Shared defer-state tracking and escalation helpers (triage + subjective) |
| `triage_start_policy.py` | `decide_triage_start()` — triage defer decision logic |
| `auto_prune.py` | Auto-cluster stale pruning helper |

---

### **KEY NUANCES & GATES**

#### **Mid-Cycle vs. Cycle Boundary**
- **Mid-cycle:** `plan_start_scores` is set (real values), queue has items
  - **Skips:** auto-clustering, triage injection, unscored injection, postflight scan completion
  - **Still runs:** subjective sync, communicate-score, create-plan, lifecycle phase
  - **Triage:** Deferred (banner shown, metadata updated) unless escalated

#### **Force-Rescan**
- Clears synthetic items, plan_start_scores, triage metadata
- Skips plan-start seeding and postflight completion
- Auto-clustering still deferred (checked via mid-cycle guard)

#### **Triage Deferral Logic**
- **Defer when:** objective backlog exists AND new review issues appeared
- **Escalate when:** same issues deferred for 3+ scans AND 7+ days
- **On escalation:** force-inject triage stages even with backlog, normalize prefixes

#### **Lifecycle Phase Resolution** (in order)
1. Has initial reviews (unscored) → REVIEW
2. Has objective backlog → EXECUTE
3. Has deferred items OR postflight scan pending → SCAN
4. Has postflight review work → REVIEW
5. Has postflight workflow items → WORKFLOW
6. Has triage stages → TRIAGE
7. Fall back to persisted or SCAN

#### **Subjective Dimension Sync**
- **Unscored:** Only injected at cycle boundaries (not mid-cycle)
- **Stale/under-target:** Injected if no backlog, evicted if backlog + not escalated, force-promoted if escalated

#### **Postflight Scan Completion Guard**
- Requires: queue empty AND no deferred disposition AND no objective backlog
- Sets `refresh_state["postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count"]`
- Cleared by objective work resumption or lifecycle reset

#### **Logging Entry Points**
- `append_log_entry(plan, actor="system", detail={...})` called from multiple sync helpers
- Each log entry records trigger, what was injected/pruned/deferred
- Review import has comprehensive log capturing all delta details

---

### **SUMMARY TABLE: Post-Scan vs. Post-Import**

| Step | Post-Scan | Post-Import | Notes |
|------|-----------|------------|-------|
| Force-rescan cycle reset | Yes | N/A | Only post-scan |
| Issue reconciliation (dead refs) | Yes | No | Only post-scan |
| Policy computation | Yes | Yes | Post-import: always, Post-scan: after reconcile |
| Subjective dimension sync | Yes | Yes | Same function, slightly different cycle context |
| Auto-clustering | Yes (mid-cycle gated) | Yes (post-import always) | Post-import always runs |
| Triage injection | Yes | Yes | Both respect mid-cycle defer |
| Workflow gates | Yes (3 gates) | Yes (3 gates) | Identical gate logic |
| Lifecycle phase sync | Yes | Yes (if dirty) | Post-import conditional on changes |
| Plan-start score seed/clear | Yes | No | Only post-scan |
| Postflight scan completion | Yes (if ready) | No | Only post-scan |
| Logging | Yes | Yes | Different entry points, Post-import more detailed |

---

    49→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    50→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    51→
    52→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    53→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    54→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    55→    """
    56→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    57→    synthetic = [item for item in order if is_synthetic_id(item)]
    58→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    59→        return False
    60→    for item in synthetic:
    61→        order.remove(item)
    62→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    63→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    64→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    65→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    66→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    67→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    68→    count = len(synthetic)
    69→    if count:
    70→        print(
    71→            colorize(
    72→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    73→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    74→                "yellow",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→    return True
    78→
    79→

    81→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    82→    return bool(
    83→        plan.get("queue_order")
    84→        or plan.get("overrides")
    85→        or plan.get("clusters")
    86→        or plan.get("skipped")

   147→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   148→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   149→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   150→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   151→        return False
   152→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   153→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   154→        return False
   155→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   156→    if scores.strict is None:
   157→        return False
   158→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   159→        "strict": scores.strict,
   160→        "overall": scores.overall,
   161→        "objective": scores.objective,
   162→        "verified": scores.verified,
   163→    }
   164→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   165→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   166→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   167→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   168→    return True

   171→def _has_objective_cycle(
   172→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   173→    plan: dict[str, object],
   174→) -> bool | None:
   175→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   176→    try:
   177→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   178→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   182→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   183→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   184→        return None
   185→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   186→
   187→
   188→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   189→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   190→) -> bool:
   191→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   192→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   193→        return False
   194→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   195→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   196→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   197→        return False
   198→
   199→    try:
   200→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   201→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   202→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   203→            score_display_mode,
   204→        )
   205→
   206→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   207→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   208→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   209→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   210→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   211→        return False
   212→    if not queue_empty:
   213→        return False
   214→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   215→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   216→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   217→    # in the next cycle.
   218→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   219→    return True

   393→        append_log_entry(
   394→            plan,
   395→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   396→            actor="system",
   397→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   398→        )
   399→    return changed
   400→
   401→
   402→def _has_postflight_review_work(
   403→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   404→    *,
   405→    policy,
   406→) -> bool:
   407→    issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   408→    has_review_like_issue = any(
   409→        isinstance(issue, dict)
   410→        and issue.get("status") == "open"
   411→        and (is_triage_finding(issue) or is_assessment_request(issue))
   412→        for issue in issues.values()
   413→    )
   414→    if has_review_like_issue:
   415→        return True
   416→    return bool(policy.stale_ids or policy.under_target_ids)
   417→
   418→
   419→def _has_postflight_workflow_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   420→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   421→    return any(
   422→        item_id in order
   423→        for item_id in (
   424→            "workflow::import-scores",
   425→            "workflow::communicate-score",
   426→            "workflow::score-checkpoint",
   427→            "workflow::create-plan",
   428→        )
   429→    )
   430→
   431→
   432→def _has_triage_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   433→    return any(
   434→        isinstance(item_id, str) and is_triage_id(item_id)
   435→        for item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(
   440→    plan: dict[str, object],
   441→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   442→) -> bool:
   443→    phase = coarse_phase_name(queue_context(state, plan=plan).snapshot.phase)
   444→    changed = set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
   445→    if changed:
   446→        append_log_entry(
   447→            plan,
   448→            "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   449→            actor="system",
   450→            detail={"phase": phase},
   451→        )
   452→    return changed
   453→
   454→
   455→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   456→    *,
   457→    plan: dict[str, object],
   458→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   459→) -> bool:
   460→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   461→    return bool(_apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan))
   462→
   463→
   464→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   465→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   466→
   467→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   468→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   469→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   470→
   471→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   472→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   473→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   474→    """
   475→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   476→        return False
   477→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   478→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   479→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   480→
   481→
   482→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   483→    *,
   484→    plan: dict[str, object],
   485→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   486→    target_strict: float,
   487→    policy,
   488→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   489→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   490→) -> bool:
   491→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   492→
   493→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   494→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   495→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   496→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   497→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   498→    """
   499→    dirty = False
   500→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   501→
   502→    if _sync_subjective_and_log(
   503→        plan,
   504→        state,
   505→        policy=policy,
   506→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   507→    ):
   508→        dirty = True
   509→    if not mid_cycle:
   510→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   511→            plan,
   512→            state,
   513→            target_strict=target_strict,
   514→            policy=policy,
   515→        ):
   516→            dirty = True
   517→    else:
   518→        print(
   519→            colorize(
   520→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   521→                "preserve queue state.",
   522→                "dim",
   523→            )
   524→        )
   525→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   526→        dirty = True
   527→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   528→        dirty = True
   529→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   530→        dirty = True
   531→    if not force_rescan:
   532→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   533→            dirty = True
   534→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   535→            dirty = True
   536→    if _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state):
   537→        dirty = True
   538→    return dirty
   539→
   540→
   541→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   542→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   543→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   544→    try:
   545→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   546→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   547→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   548→        return
   549→
   550→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   551→
   552→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   553→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   554→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   555→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   556→
   557→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   558→
   559→    # Policy must be computed after reconciliation, which mutates plan
   560→    # (supersede/prune resolved issues) before policy reads it.
   561→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   562→        runtime,
   563→        plan,
   564→    )
   565→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   566→        plan=plan,
   567→        state=runtime.state,
   568→        target_strict=target_strict,
   569→        policy=policy,
   570→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   571→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   572→    ) or dirty
   573→
   574→    if dirty:
   575→        try:
   576→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   577→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   578→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   579→
```

   379→
   380→def sync_plan_after_import(
   381→    state: dict,
   382→    diff: dict,
   383→    assessment_mode: str,
   384→    *,
   385→    request: PlanImportSyncRequest | None = None,
   386→) -> None:
   387→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
   388→    try:
   389→        state_file = request.state_file if request is not None else None
   390→        config = request.config if request is not None else None
   391→        import_file = request.import_file if request is not None else None
   392→        import_payload = request.import_payload if request is not None else None
   393→
   394→        plan_path = None
   395→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   396→        if state_file is not None:
   397→            plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   398→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   399→            return
   400→
   401→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   402→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   403→            state,
   404→            target_strict=target_strict,
   405→            plan=plan,
   406→        )
   407→        snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
   408→        current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   409→            strict=snapshot.strict,
   410→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   411→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   412→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   413→        )
   414→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   415→        communicate_result = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   416→            plan,
   417→            state,
   418→            policy=policy,
   419→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   420→            current_scores=current_scores,
   421→        )
   422→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   423→            plan,
   424→            state,
   425→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   426→            import_file=import_file,
   427→            import_payload=import_payload,
   428→        )
   429→        create_plan_result = sync_create_plan_needed(
   430→            plan,
   431→            state,
   432→            policy=policy,
   433→        )
   434→
   435→        sync_inputs = _build_import_sync_inputs(diff, import_payload)
   436→        mutations = _PlanImportMutations()
   437→        _record_workflow_change(
   438→            mutations,
   439→            communicate_result,
   440→            workflow_id="workflow::communicate-score",
   441→        )
   442→        _record_workflow_change(
   443→            mutations,
   444→            import_scores_result,
   445→            workflow_id="workflow::import-scores",
   446→            injected=bool(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", ())),
   447→        )
   448→        _record_workflow_change(
   449→            mutations,
   450→            create_plan_result,
   451→            workflow_id="workflow::create-plan",
   452→        )
   453→        _sync_review_delta(
   454→            mutations,
   455→            plan,
   456→            state,
   457→            policy=policy,
   458→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   459→        )
   460→        _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   461→            mutations,
   462→            plan,
   463→            state,
   464→            policy=policy,
   465→            target_strict=target_strict,
   466→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   467→        )
   468→
   469→        if mutations.dirty:
   470→            if _sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan, state, policy=policy):
   471→                mutations.dirty = True
   472→            _append_workflow_log_entries(
   473→                plan,
   474→                communicate_result=communicate_result,
   475→                import_scores_result=import_scores_result,
   476→                create_plan_result=create_plan_result,
   477→            )
   478→            _append_review_import_sync_log(
   479→                plan,
   480→                diff,
   481→                mutations,
   482→                covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids,
   483→            )
   484→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   485→
   486→        if mutations.import_result is not None:
   487→            _print_review_import_sync(
   488→                state,
   489→                mutations.import_result,
   490→                workflow_injected=bool(mutations.workflow_injected_ids),
   491→            )
   492→        _print_workflow_injected_message(mutations.workflow_injected_ids)
   493→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   494→        print(
   495→            colorize(
   496→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   497→                "dim",
   498→            )
   499→        )
   500→
   501→
   502→__all__ = ["PlanImportSyncRequest", "sync_plan_after_import"]
   503→

1→"""Plan sync helpers for review-import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    10→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    11→    compute_open_issue_ids,
    12→    sync_triage_needed,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import is_triage_finding
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
    20→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
    21→
    22→    new_ids: set[str]
    23→    added_to_queue: list[str]
    24→    triage_injected: bool
    25→    stale_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    26→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    27→    triage_deferred: bool = False

    30→def _has_triage_baseline(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    31→    """Return True when triage has recorded at least one baseline issue ID."""
    32→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    33→    triaged_ids = meta.get("triaged_ids", [])
    34→    return bool(triaged_ids)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
    38→    plan: PlanModel,
    39→    state: StateModel,
    40→    *,
    41→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    42→) -> set[str]:
    43→    """Return review IDs that should be synced into queue_order after import.
    44→
    45→    No triage baseline yet: include all currently-open review IDs so the
    46→    first import cannot drop follow-up work.
    47→    Existing triage baseline: include only IDs that are new since triage.
    48→    """
    49→    if _has_triage_baseline(plan):
    50→        return compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
    51→    return set(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else compute_open_issue_ids(state)
    52→
    53→
    54→def _is_review_queue_id(issue_id: str, state: StateModel) -> bool:
    55→    """Return True for queue IDs representing triage findings.
    56→
    57→    Falls back to legacy ID prefixes only when the issue payload is absent.
    58→    """
    59→    issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(issue_id)
    60→    if isinstance(issue, dict):
    61→        return is_triage_finding(issue)
    62→    return issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::")
    63→
    64→
    65→def _prune_stale_triage_meta(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    *,
    68→    stale_ids: set[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Drop stale review IDs from triage recovery metadata."""
    71→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    72→    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not stale_ids:
    73→        return
    74→
    75→    active_ids = meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")
    76→    if isinstance(active_ids, list):
    77→        filtered_active = [
    78→            issue_id for issue_id in active_ids
    79→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    80→        ]
    81→        if filtered_active:
    82→            meta["active_triage_issue_ids"] = filtered_active
    83→        else:
    84→            meta.pop("active_triage_issue_ids", None)
    85→
    86→    undispositioned_ids = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_ids")
    87→    if isinstance(undispositioned_ids, list):
    88→        filtered_undispositioned = [
    89→            issue_id for issue_id in undispositioned_ids
    90→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    91→        ]
    92→        if filtered_undispositioned:
    93→            meta["undispositioned_issue_ids"] = filtered_undispositioned
    94→            meta["undispositioned_issue_count"] = len(filtered_undispositioned)
    95→        else:
    96→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_ids", None)
    97→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_count", None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel,
   103→    *,
   104→    live_open_review_ids: set[str],
   105→) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Remove stale review IDs from queue-related plan containers.
   107→
   108→    Review imports can auto-resolve prior review IDs. These IDs must be removed
   109→    from queue_order (and linked queue metadata) or the queue can drift from
   110→    state reality.
   111→    """
   112→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   113→    stale_ids = sorted(
   114→        {
   115→            issue_id
   116→            for issue_id in order
   117→            if _is_review_queue_id(issue_id, state) and issue_id not in live_open_review_ids
   118→        }
   119→    )
   120→    if not stale_ids:
   121→        return []
   122→
   123→    stale_set = set(stale_ids)
   124→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in stale_set]
   125→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, stale_set)
   126→    _prune_stale_triage_meta(plan, stale_ids=stale_set)
   127→
   128→    deferred = plan.get("deferred")
   129→    if isinstance(deferred, list):
   130→        plan["deferred"] = [issue_id for issue_id in deferred if issue_id not in stale_set]
   131→
   132→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
   133→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   134→        for issue_id in stale_set:
   135→            skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   136→
   137→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   138→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
   139→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   140→            continue
   141→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
   142→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in stale_set
   143→        ]
   144→
   145→    return stale_ids
   146→
   147→
   148→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→    *,
   152→    policy=None,
   153→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   154→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   155→
   156→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order, prunes stale review IDs from queue
   157→    containers, and injects triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard
   158→    — defers when objective work remains). Returns ``None`` when no queue
   159→    changes are required.
   160→    """
   161→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   162→    open_review_ids = compute_open_issue_ids(state)
   163→    stale_pruned_from_queue = _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   164→        plan,
   165→        state,
   166→        live_open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   167→    )
   168→    new_ids = _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
   169→        plan,
   170→        state,
   171→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   172→    )
   173→    if not new_ids and not stale_pruned_from_queue:
   174→        return None
   175→
   176→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   177→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   178→    existing = set(order)
   179→    added: list[str] = []
   180→    for issue_id in sorted(new_ids):
   181→        if issue_id not in existing:
   182→            order.append(issue_id)
   183→            added.append(issue_id)
   184→
   185→    # Inject triage stages if needed (policy enables mid-cycle guard)
   186→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   187→    triage_injected_ids = list(getattr(triage_result, "injected", []) or [])
   188→    triage_injected = bool(triage_injected_ids)
   189→    triage_deferred = bool(triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "deferred", False))
   190→
   191→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   192→        new_ids=new_ids,
   193→        added_to_queue=added,
   194→        stale_pruned_from_queue=stale_pruned_from_queue,
   195→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   196→        triage_injected_ids=triage_injected_ids,
   197→        triage_deferred=triage_deferred,
   198→    )
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["ReviewImportSyncResult", "sync_plan_after_review_import"]
   202→

1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether a planned objective backlog exists.
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available (plan-aware:
    32→    only counts planned objectives post-triage).  Falls back to scanning
    33→    *state_or_issues* directly (counts all open non-subjective issues).
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"work_items": ...}`) or a raw
    39→    # work-item mapping, with legacy ``issues`` as a fallback alias.
    40→    issues = state_or_issues
    41→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    42→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("work_items")
    43→        if not isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    44→            maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    45→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    46→            issues = maybe_issues
    47→    return any(
    48→        f.get("status") == "open"
    49→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    50→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    51→        for f in issues.values()
    52→    )
    53→
    54→
    55→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    56→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    57→
    58→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    59→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    60→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    61→    """
    62→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    63→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    64→
    65→
    66→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    67→
```

1→"""Triage sync — inject/prune triage stage IDs based on review issue changes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     7→    TRIAGE_IDS,
     8→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
     9→    QueueSyncResult,
    10→    confirmed_triage_stage_names,
    11→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix,
    12→    recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→
    18→from .defer_policy import (
    19→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    20→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    21→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    22→    update_defer_state,
    23→)
    24→from .triage_start_policy import decide_triage_start
    25→
    26→[REDACTED]
    27→_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    28→[REDACTED]
    29→
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Helpers
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→def _new_review_ids_since_triage(
    36→    state: StateModel,
    37→    meta: dict,
    38→) -> set[str]:
    39→    """Return review issue IDs that are new since the last triage."""
    40→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    41→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    42→    known_ids = triaged_ids | active_ids
    43→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state) - known_ids if known_ids else set()
    44→
    45→
    46→def _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta: dict) -> set[str]:
    47→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    48→    active_ids = set(meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids", []))
    49→    return triaged_ids | active_ids
    50→
    51→
    52→def _prune_all_triage_stages(order: list[str]) -> None:
    53→    """Remove all ``triage::*`` stage IDs from *order*."""
    54→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    55→        while sid in order:
    56→            order.remove(sid)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _inject_pending_triage_stages(
    60→    order: list[str],
    61→    confirmed: set[str],
    62→    *,
    63→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    64→) -> list[str]:
    65→    """Inject triage stages for pending (unconfirmed) items.
    66→
    67→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
    68→    Returns list of injected stage IDs.
    69→    """
    70→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    71→    existing = set(order)
    72→    injected: list[str] = []
    73→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    74→        if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
    75→            if skipped is not None:
    76→                skipped.pop(sid, None)
    77→            order.append(sid)
    78→            injected.append(sid)
    79→            existing.add(sid)
    80→    return injected
    81→
    82→
    83→def _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta: dict) -> None:
    84→    """Clear defer metadata once triage is no longer deferred."""
    85→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    86→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _store_triage_meta(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
    90→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    91→
    92→
    93→def _inject_triage_result(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    confirmed: set[str],
    96→    *,
    97→    skipped: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    98→    normalize: bool = False,
    99→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   100→    injected = _inject_pending_triage_stages(order, confirmed, skipped=skipped)
   101→    if not injected:
   102→        return QueueSyncResult()
   103→    if normalize:
   104→        normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   105→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=injected)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _mark_triage_deferred(
   109→    plan: PlanModel,
   110→    meta: dict,
   111→    *,
   112→    defer_state: dict,
   113→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   114→    meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   115→    meta["triage_recommended"] = True
   116→    meta.pop(_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY, None)
   117→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   118→    return QueueSyncResult(deferred=True)
   119→
   120→
   121→def _mark_triage_escalated(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   122→    meta[_TRIAGE_FORCE_VISIBLE_KEY] = True
   123→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   124→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   125→
   126→
   127→def _mark_triage_ready(plan: PlanModel, meta: dict) -> None:
   128→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   129→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   130→    _store_triage_meta(plan, meta)
   131→
   132→
   133→def _prune_stale_present_stages(
   134→    *,
   135→    plan: PlanModel,
   136→    state: StateModel,
   137→    order: list[str],
   138→    meta: dict,
   139→    last_hash: str,
   140→    confirmed: set[str],
   141→    recorded_unconfirmed: set[str],
   142→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   143→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   144→    if not last_hash or confirmed or recorded_unconfirmed:
   145→        return result
   146→    if not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta) and stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state):
   147→        return result
   148→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   149→    if new_since_triage:
   150→        return result
   151→    _prune_all_triage_stages(order)
   152→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   153→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   154→    if current_hash:
   155→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   156→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   157→    result.pruned = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
   158→    return result
   159→
   160→
   161→def _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   162→    *,
   163→    plan: PlanModel,
   164→    state: StateModel,
   165→    meta: dict,
   166→    last_hash: str,
   167→) -> None:
   168→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   169→    has_completed_stage = any(
   170→        isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("confirmed_at")
   171→        for v in stages.values()
   172→    )
   173→    if not has_completed_stage or meta.get("triaged_ids") or last_hash:
   174→        return
   175→    current_review = sorted(stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state))
   176→    if current_review:
   177→        meta["triaged_ids"] = current_review
   178→        meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   179→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   180→
   181→
   182→def _defer_or_inject_triage(
   183→    *,
   184→    plan: PlanModel,
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    order: list[str],
   187→    meta: dict,
   188→    confirmed: set[str],
   189→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   190→    new_since_triage: set[str],
   191→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   192→    decision = decide_triage_start(
   193→        plan,
   194→        state,
   195→        policy=policy,
   196→        explicit_start=False,
   197→        attested_override=False,
   198→    )
   199→    if decision.action == "defer":
   200→        defer_state = update_defer_state(
   201→            meta.get(_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   202→            state=state,
   203→            deferred_ids=new_since_triage,
   204→            options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   205→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   206→            ),
   207→        )
   208→        meta[_TRIAGE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   209→        escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   210→            defer_state,
   211→            state=state,
   212→            options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   213→                deferred_ids_field=_TRIAGE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   214→            ),
   215→        )
   216→        if not escalated:
   217→            return _mark_triage_deferred(plan, meta, defer_state=defer_state)
   218→        _mark_triage_escalated(plan, meta)
   219→        return _inject_triage_result(
   220→            order,
   221→            confirmed,
   222→            skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   223→            normalize=True,
   224→        )
   225→
   226→    _mark_triage_ready(plan, meta)
   227→    return _inject_triage_result(
   228→        order,
   229→        confirmed,
   230→        skipped=plan.get("skipped", {}),
   231→    )
   232→
   233→
   234→def _sync_hash_change(
   235→    *,
   236→    plan: PlanModel,
   237→    state: StateModel,
   238→    order: list[str],
   239→    meta: dict,
   240→    confirmed: set[str],
   241→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   242→    current_hash: str,
   243→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   244→    new_since_triage = _new_review_ids_since_triage(state, meta)
   245→    if not new_since_triage and not _baseline_triage_issue_ids(meta):
   246→        new_since_triage = stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   247→    if new_since_triage:
   248→        return _defer_or_inject_triage(
   249→            plan=plan,
   250→            state=state,
   251→            order=order,
   252→            meta=meta,
   253→            confirmed=confirmed,
   254→            policy=policy,
   255→            new_since_triage=new_since_triage,
   256→        )
   257→
   258→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   259→    meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
   260→    _clear_triage_defer_tracking(meta)
   261→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   262→    return QueueSyncResult()
   263→
   264→
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→# Public API
   267→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   268→
   269→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   270→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   271→
   272→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   273→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   274→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   275→    resolved since then.
   276→
   277→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   278→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   279→    through the plan.
   280→    """
   281→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   282→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state)
   283→
   284→
   285→def compute_open_issue_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   286→    """Return the set of currently-open review/concerns issue IDs."""
   287→    return stale_policy_mod.open_review_ids(state)
   288→
   289→
   290→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   291→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   292→
   293→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   294→    """
   295→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   296→
   297→
   298→def sync_triage_needed(
   299→    plan: PlanModel,
   300→    state: StateModel,
   301→    *,
   302→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   303→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   304→    """Append triage stage IDs to back of queue when review issues change.
   305→
   306→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   307→
   308→    **Mid-cycle guard**: when the objective backlog still has work, triage
   309→    stages are NOT injected.  Instead, ``epic_triage_meta["triage_recommended"]``
   310→    is set so the UI can show a non-blocking banner.  Stages are injected
   311→    once the objective backlog drains (or on manual ``plan triage``). If
   312→    deferral repeats for multiple scans/days, triage escalates and stages are
   313→    injected in workflow/triage priority order with a forced-visibility marker.
   314→
   315→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   316→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   317→
   318→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   319→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   320→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   321→    """
   322→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   323→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   324→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   325→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   326→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   327→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   328→
   329→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   330→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   331→
   332→    current_hash = stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   333→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   334→
   335→    if already_present:
   336→        return _prune_stale_present_stages(
   337→            plan=plan,
   338→            state=state,
   339→            order=order,
   340→            meta=meta,
   341→            last_hash=last_hash,
   342→            confirmed=confirmed,
   343→            recorded_unconfirmed=recorded_unconfirmed,
   344→        )
   345→
   346→    _backfill_partial_triage_snapshot(
   347→        plan=plan,
   348→        state=state,
   349→        meta=meta,
   350→        last_hash=last_hash,
   351→    )
   352→
   353→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   354→        return _sync_hash_change(
   355→            plan=plan,
   356→            state=state,
   357→            order=order,
   358→            meta=meta,
   359→            confirmed=confirmed,
   360→            policy=policy,
   361→            current_hash=current_hash,
   362→        )
   363→
   364→    return result
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "compute_open_issue_ids",
   369→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   370→    "is_triage_stale",
   371→    "sync_triage_needed",
   372→]
   373→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Single-pass sync for all three subjective categories:
     4→
     5→- **unscored** — placeholder dimensions never yet reviewed.  Injected at cycle
     6→  boundaries only; pruned mid-cycle.
     7→- **stale** — dimensions whose assessment needs a review refresh.
     8→- **under_target** — scored below target but not stale.
     9→
    10→Stale and under-target IDs share injection rules: injected when no objective
    11→backlog exists, evicted when it does, force-promoted after repeated deferrals.
    12→
    13→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue
    14→(except escalation promotion, which moves deferred IDs ahead of objective work).
    15→"""
    16→
    17→from __future__ import annotations
    18→
    19→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    21→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    22→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    23→    QueueSyncResult,
    24→    is_triage_id,
    25→    is_workflow_id,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    28→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    29→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    30→
    31→from .context import has_objective_backlog, is_mid_cycle
    32→from .defer_policy import (
    33→    DeferEscalationOptions,
    34→    DeferUpdateOptions,
    35→    should_escalate_defer_state,
    36→    update_defer_state,
    37→)
    38→
    39→
    40→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→# Defer-meta keys (shared with work_queue readers via plan dict)
    42→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    43→
    44→[REDACTED]
    45→_DEFER_IDS_FIELD = "deferred_review_ids"
    46→[REDACTED]
    47→
    48→
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50→# ID helpers
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→
    53→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    54→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    55→
    56→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    57→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    58→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    59→    """
    60→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    61→        state,
    62→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    63→    )
    64→
    65→
    66→def current_under_target_ids(
    67→    state: StateModel,
    68→    *,
    69→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    70→) -> set[str]:
    71→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    72→
    73→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    74→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    75→    """
    76→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    77→        state,
    78→        target_strict=target_strict,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    84→# Helpers
    85→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    86→
    87→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    88→    order: list[str],
    89→    *,
    90→    keep_ids: set[str],
    91→    pruned: list[str],
    92→) -> None:
    93→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    94→    to_remove = [
    95→        fid for fid in order
    96→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    97→        and fid not in keep_ids
    98→    ]
    99→    for fid in to_remove:
   100→        order.remove(fid)
   101→        pruned.append(fid)
   102→
   103→
   104→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
   105→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
   106→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   107→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   108→        return set()
   109→    return {
   110→        str(fid)
   111→        for fid in skipped
   112→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   113→    }
   114→
   115→
   116→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
   117→    order: list[str],
   118→    *,
   119→    skipped_ids: set[str],
   120→    pruned: list[str],
   121→) -> None:
   122→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   123→    if not skipped_ids:
   124→        return
   125→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   126→    for fid in to_remove:
   127→        order.remove(fid)
   128→        pruned.append(fid)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   132→    order: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    inject_ids: set[str],
   135→    injected: list[str],
   136→) -> None:
   137→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   138→
   139→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   140→    """
   141→    existing = set(order)
   142→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   143→        if sid not in existing:
   144→            order.append(sid)
   145→            injected.append(sid)
   146→
   147→
   148→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→# Deferral tracking and escalation
   150→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→def _clear_defer_meta(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   153→    plan.pop(_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
   154→
   155→
   156→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
   157→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order.
   158→
   159→    Inserts after any workflow/triage items at the front of the queue.
   160→    """
   161→    target_ids: list[str] = []
   162→    seen: set[str] = set()
   163→    for fid in ids:
   164→        sid = str(fid).strip()
   165→        if not sid or sid in seen:
   166→            continue
   167→        target_ids.append(sid)
   168→        seen.add(sid)
   169→    if not target_ids:
   170→        return 0
   171→
   172→    insert_at = 0
   173→    while insert_at < len(order):
   174→        current = str(order[insert_at])
   175→        if not (is_workflow_id(current) or is_triage_id(current)):
   176→            break
   177→        insert_at += 1
   178→
   179→    changes = 0
   180→    for sid in target_ids:
   181→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   182→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   183→        if existing_idx is not None:
   184→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   185→                insert_at += 1
   186→                continue
   187→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   188→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   189→                target_idx -= 1
   190→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   191→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   192→        changes += 1
   193→    return changes
   194→
   195→
   196→def _update_deferral(
   197→    plan: PlanModel,
   198→    state: StateModel,
   199→    *,
   200→    injectable_ids: set[str],
   201→    stale_ids: set[str],
   202→    under_target_ids: set[str],
   203→    skipped_ids: set[str],
   204→) -> bool:
   205→    """Update defer-meta and return True if escalation threshold is reached."""
   206→    stale_deferred = sorted(stale_ids - skipped_ids)
   207→    under_target_deferred = sorted(under_target_ids - skipped_ids)
   208→
   209→    defer_state = update_defer_state(
   210→        plan.get(_DEFER_META_KEY),
   211→        state=state,
   212→        deferred_ids=injectable_ids,
   213→        options=DeferUpdateOptions(deferred_ids_field=_DEFER_IDS_FIELD),
   214→    )
   215→    defer_state["deferred_stale_ids"] = stale_deferred
   216→    defer_state["deferred_under_target_ids"] = under_target_deferred
   217→
   218→    escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   219→        defer_state,
   220→        state=state,
   221→        options=DeferEscalationOptions(deferred_ids_field=_DEFER_IDS_FIELD),
   222→    )
   223→
   224→    if escalated:
   225→        defer_state[_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = sorted(injectable_ids)
   226→    else:
   227→        defer_state.pop(_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
   228→
   229→    plan[_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   230→    return escalated
   231→
   232→
   233→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   234→# Unified subjective dimension sync
   235→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   236→
   237→def sync_subjective_dimensions(
   238→    plan: PlanModel,
   239→    state: StateModel,
   240→    *,
   241→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   242→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   243→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   244→    """Single-pass sync for all subjective dimensions in the plan queue.
   245→
   246→    Handles three categories with unified prune/inject logic:
   247→
   248→    - **unscored**: placeholder dimensions not yet reviewed.  Injected at cycle
   249→      boundaries; pruned mid-cycle.
   250→    - **stale**: dimensions needing review refresh.
   251→    - **under_target**: scored below target but not stale.
   252→
   253→    Stale/under-target injection is gated by objective backlog:
   254→
   255→    1. **No backlog** (or cycle just completed) — inject to back of queue.
   256→    2. **Backlog, not escalated** — evict from queue.  Update deferral counter.
   257→    3. **Backlog, escalated** — force-promote to front (after workflow/triage).
   258→
   259→    Unscored injection is unconditional at cycle boundaries, independent of
   260→    backlog state.
   261→    """
   262→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   263→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   264→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   265→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   266→
   267→    # --- Compute all ID sets once -----------------------------------------
   268→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   269→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   270→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   271→    )
   272→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   273→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   274→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   275→
   276→    # --- Resurface: clear skips for never-reviewed dimensions -------------
   277→    if not mid_cycle:
   278→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   279→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   280→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   281→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   282→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   283→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   284→
   285→    # --- Repair: remove skipped IDs from queue_order ----------------------
   286→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   287→
   288→    # --- Backlog and deferral ---------------------------------------------
   289→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   290→    should_defer = objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed
   291→    escalated = False
   292→    if should_defer and injectable_ids:
   293→        escalated = _update_deferral(
   294→            plan, state,
   295→            injectable_ids=injectable_ids,
   296→            stale_ids=stale_ids,
   297→            under_target_ids=under_target_ids,
   298→            skipped_ids=skipped_ids,
   299→        )
   300→    else:
   301→        _clear_defer_meta(plan)
   302→
   303→    # --- Single prune pass ------------------------------------------------
   304→    evicting = should_defer and not escalated
   305→    if evicting:
   306→        keep_ids = set() if mid_cycle else unscored_ids
   307→    else:
   308→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if mid_cycle else (unscored_ids | injectable_ids)
   309→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   310→
   311→    # --- Inject unscored (cycle boundaries only) --------------------------
   312→    if not mid_cycle:
   313→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   314→            order,
   315→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   316→            injected=result.injected,
   317→        )
   318→
   319→    # --- Inject or promote stale/under_target -----------------------------
   320→    if escalated:
   321→        _promote_subjective_ids(order, sorted(injectable_ids))
   322→    elif not evicting and injectable_ids:
   323→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   324→
   325→    return result
   326→

    79→def _latest_assessment_audit(

   117→    if not packet_sha256 and isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict):

   119→    return PendingImportScoresMeta(
   120→        timestamp=timestamp,
   121→        import_file=recorded_file,
   122→        normalized_import_file=_normalize_match_path(recorded_file) or "",
   123→        packet_sha256=packet_sha256,

   127→def pending_import_scores_meta(
   128→    plan: PlanModel,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> PendingImportScoresMeta | None:
   131→    """Return queued score-import metadata without mutating the plan."""

   133→    if refresh_state is not None:
   134→        meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   135→            refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   136→        )
   137→        if meta is not None:
   138→            return meta
   139→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   140→    if issues_only_audit is None:
   141→        return None
   142→    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(

   149→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   150→    meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | dict[str, Any] | None,
   151→    *,
   152→    import_file: str,
   153→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   154→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   155→    """Return (matches, reason) for whether the import matches the pending batch.
   156→
   157→    Checks packet_sha256 first (strongest signal), falls back to normalized
   158→    file path.  Returns a single human-readable reason on mismatch.
   159→    """
   160→    normalized_meta = (
   161→        meta
   162→        if isinstance(meta, PendingImportScoresMeta)
   163→        else PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(meta)
   164→    )
   165→    if normalized_meta is None:
   166→        return True, ""
   167→
   168→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   169→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   170→
   171→    expected_hash = normalized_meta.packet_sha256
   172→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   173→    if expected_hash and current_hash:
   174→        if current_hash == expected_hash:
   175→            return True, ""
   176→        return False, f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash}"
   177→
   178→    expected_file = normalized_meta.normalized_import_file
   179→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   180→    if expected_file and current_file and current_file != expected_file:
   181→        return False, f"expected import file {normalized_meta.import_file}, got {import_file}"
   182→
   183→    return True, ""
   184→

   187→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   188→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   189→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   190→    refresh_state = _get_refresh_state(plan)
   191→    if refresh_state is not None:
   192→        refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
   193→

   196→    pending_meta: PendingImportScoresMeta | None,
   197→    latest_issues_only: dict[str, Any],
   198→) -> str:
   199→    if pending_meta is not None and pending_meta.timestamp:
   200→        return pending_meta.timestamp

   249→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   250→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   251→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   252→    if item_id not in order:
   253→        order.append(item_id)
   254→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   255→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   256→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   257→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   258→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])

   264→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   265→    plan: PlanModel,
   266→    state: StateModel,

   269→) -> QueueSyncResult:

   274→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   275→

   279→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   280→

   288→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   289→    plan: PlanModel,
   290→    state: StateModel,
   291→    *,
   292→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   293→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   294→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   295→
   296→    Only injects when:
   297→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   298→    - At least one objective issue exists
   299→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   300→    - No triage stages are pending
   301→
   302→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   303→    """

   305→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   306→
   307→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:

   311→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):

   313→
