   319→
   320→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   321→    plan: PlanModel,
   322→    state: StateModel,
   323→    *,
   324→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   325→    import_file: str | None = None,
   326→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   327→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   328→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   329→
   330→    Only injects when:
   331→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   332→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   333→
   334→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   335→    """
   336→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   337→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   338→    refresh_state = _ensure_refresh_state(plan)
   339→    pending_meta = PendingImportScoresMeta.from_mapping(
   340→        refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   341→    )
   342→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   343→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   344→
   345→    if _pending_import_scores_stale(
   346→        order=order,
   347→        pending_meta=pending_meta,
   348→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   349→        latest_trusted=latest_trusted,
   350→    ):
   351→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   352→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   353→
   354→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   355→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   356→            _record_pending_import_scores(
   357→                refresh_state,
   358→                import_file=import_file,
   359→                import_payload=import_payload,
   360→                latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   361→            )
   362→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   363→        return _EMPTY()
   364→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   365→        return _EMPTY()
   366→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   367→    _record_pending_import_scores(
   368→        refresh_state,
   369→        import_file=import_file,
   370→        import_payload=import_payload,
   371→        latest_issues_only=latest_issues_only,
   372→    )
   373→    return result

   375→
   376→class ScoreSnapshot:
   377→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   378→
   379→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   380→
   381→    def __init__(
   382→        self,
   383→        *,
   384→        strict: float | None,
   385→        overall: float | None,
   386→        objective: float | None,
   387→        verified: float | None,
   388→    ) -> None:
   389→        self.strict = strict
   390→        self.overall = overall
   391→        self.objective = objective
   392→        self.verified = verified
   393→
   394→
   395→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   396→    plan: PlanModel,
   397→    state: StateModel,
   398→    *,
   399→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   400→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   401→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   402→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   403→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   404→
   405→    Injects when:
   406→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims), OR
   407→      scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   408→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   409→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   410→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent), unless a trusted score import
   411→      explicitly refreshed the live score mid-cycle
   412→
   413→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   414→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   415→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   416→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   417→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   418→    re-inject.
   419→    """
   420→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   421→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   422→
   423→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   424→        return _EMPTY()
   425→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   426→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   427→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan and not scores_just_imported:
   428→        return _EMPTY()
   429→    if not scores_just_imported and not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   430→        return _EMPTY()
   431→
   432→    if current_scores is not None:
   433→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   434→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   435→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   436→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   437→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   438→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   439→
   440→
   441→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   442→    plan: PlanModel,
   443→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   444→) -> None:
   445→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   446→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   447→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   448→        return
   449→    if scores.strict is None:
   450→        return
   451→
   452→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   453→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   454→        "strict": scores.strict,
   455→        "overall": scores.overall,
   456→        "objective": scores.objective,
   457→        "verified": scores.verified,
   458→    }
   459→
   460→

   462→    "PendingImportScoresMeta",
   463→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   464→    "import_scores_meta_matches",

   466→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   467→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   468→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   469→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   470→]
   471→

1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import counts_toward_objective_backlog
    16→
    17→[REDACTED]
    18→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    19→
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    24→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    25→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    26→    {
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    31→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    32→    }
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    37→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    38→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    39→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    40→        refresh_state = {}
    41→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    42→    return refresh_state
    43→
    44→
    45→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    46→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _touches_objective_issue(
    50→    *,
    51→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None,
    52→    state: dict[str, object] | None,
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    if issue_ids is None:
    55→        return True
    56→
    57→    real_issue_ids = [
    58→        issue_id
    59→        for issue_id in issue_ids
    60→        if _is_real_queue_issue(str(issue_id))
    61→    ]
    62→    if not real_issue_ids:
    63→        return False
    64→    if not isinstance(state, dict):
    65→        return True
    66→
    67→    issues = state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})
    68→    if not isinstance(issues, dict):
    69→        return True
    70→
    71→    objective_seen = False
    72→    for issue_id in real_issue_ids:
    73→        issue = issues.get(issue_id)
    74→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    75→            return True
    76→        if counts_toward_objective_backlog(issue):
    77→            objective_seen = True
    78→    return objective_seen
    79→
    80→
    81→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    82→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    83→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    84→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    85→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    86→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    87→            return phase
    88→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    89→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    90→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    91→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    92→    return None
    93→
    94→
    95→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    96→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    97→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    98→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    99→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   100→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
   101→        return False
   102→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
   107→    plan: PlanModel,
   108→    *,
   109→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   110→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   111→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   112→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   113→    has_triage: bool,
   114→    has_deferred: bool,
   115→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
   116→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
   117→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
   118→        plan,
   119→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
   120→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
   121→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
   122→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
   123→        has_triage=has_triage,
   124→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
   125→    )
   126→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
   127→
   128→
   129→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    *,
   132→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   133→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   134→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   135→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   136→    has_triage: bool,
   137→    has_deferred: bool,
   138→) -> str:
   139→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   140→    if has_initial_reviews:
   141→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   142→    if has_objective_backlog:
   143→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   144→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   145→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   146→    if has_postflight_review:
   147→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   148→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   149→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   150→    if has_triage:
   151→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   152→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   153→    if persisted is not None:
   154→        return persisted
   155→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   156→
   157→
   158→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   159→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   160→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   161→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   162→        return True
   163→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   164→
   165→
   166→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   167→    plan: PlanModel,
   168→    *,
   169→    scan_count: int | None,
   170→) -> bool:
   171→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   172→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   173→    try:
   174→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   175→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   176→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   177→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   178→        return False
   179→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   180→    return True
   181→
   182→
   183→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   184→    plan: PlanModel,
   185→    *,
   186→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   187→    state: dict[str, object] | None = None,
   188→) -> bool:
   189→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on objective issues."""
   190→    if not _touches_objective_issue(issue_ids=issue_ids, state=state):
   191→        return False
   192→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   193→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   194→        return False
   195→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   196→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   197→    return True
   198→
   199→
   200→__all__ = [
   201→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   202→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   203→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",
   204→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW",
   205→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN",
   206→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE",
   207→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW",
   208→    "mark_postflight_scan_completed",
   209→    "postflight_scan_pending",
   210→    "resolve_lifecycle_phase",
   211→    "set_lifecycle_phase",
   212→    "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   213→    "VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES",
   214→]
   215→

    29→
    30→@dataclass
    31→class ReconcileResult:
    32→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    33→
    34→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    37→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    38→    changes: int = 0

    40→
    41→def _find_candidates(
    42→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    43→) -> list[str]:
    44→    """Find alive issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    45→    candidates: list[str] = []
    46→    for fid, issue in (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).items():
    47→        if issue.get("status") not in _ALIVE_STATUSES:
    48→            continue
    49→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    50→            candidates.append(fid)
    51→    return candidates
    52→
    53→
    54→_ALIVE_STATUSES = frozenset({"open", "deferred", "triaged_out"})
    55→
    56→
    57→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Return True if the issue exists and is actionable (open/deferred/triaged_out)."""
    59→    issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(issue_id)
    60→    if issue is None:
    61→        return False
    62→    return issue.get("status") in _ALIVE_STATUSES
    63→
    64→
    65→def _supersede_id(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    state: StateModel,
    68→    issue_id: str,
    69→    now: str,
    70→) -> bool:
    71→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    72→    issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(issue_id)
    73→    detector = ""
    74→    file = ""
    75→    summary = ""
    76→    if issue:
    77→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    78→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    79→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    80→
    81→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    82→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    83→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    84→
    85→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    86→        "original_id": issue_id,
    87→        "original_detector": detector,
    88→        "original_file": file,
    89→        "original_summary": summary,
    90→        "status": "superseded",
    91→        "superseded_at": now,
    92→        "remapped_to": None,
    93→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    # Preserve any existing override note
    97→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    98→    if override_note:
    99→        entry["note"] = override_note
   100→

   124→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   125→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   126→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   127→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   128→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   129→
   130→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   131→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   132→        try:
   133→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   134→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   135→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   136→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   137→                to_prune.append(fid)
   138→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   139→            to_prune.append(fid)
   140→
   141→    for fid in to_prune:
   142→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   143→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   144→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   145→
   146→    return to_prune
   147→
   148→
   149→def _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
   150→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   151→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   152→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   153→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
   154→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   155→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   156→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   157→    return {
   158→        fid for fid in referenced_ids - already_superseded
   159→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   160→    }
   161→
   162→
   163→def _supersede_dead_references(
   164→    plan: PlanModel,
   165→    state: StateModel,
   166→    *,
   167→    referenced_ids: set[str],
   168→    now: str,
   169→    result: ReconcileResult,
   170→) -> None:
   171→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   172→        if _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   173→            continue
   174→        if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   175→            result.superseded.append(fid)
   176→            result.changes += 1

   184→) -> None:

   205→    plan: PlanModel,

   210→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})

   213→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):

   217→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   218→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   219→            result.changes += 1
   220→        if not alive_ids:

   222→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   223→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   224→        result.changes += 1

245→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   246→    plan: PlanModel,
   247→    state: StateModel,
   248→) -> ReconcileResult:
   249→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   250→
   251→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   252→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   253→    """
   254→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   255→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   256→    result = ReconcileResult()
   257→    now = utc_now()
   258→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   259→
   260→    referenced_ids = _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan)
   261→
   262→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   263→    # clusters that become empty after state reconciliation.
   264→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   265→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   266→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   267→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   268→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   269→    }
   270→
   271→    # Sync state status for issues in plan.skipped that are still "open" in state.
   272→    # This migrates existing data: temporary skips → deferred, triaged_out skips → triaged_out.
   273→    _sync_skipped_issue_statuses(plan, state)
   274→
   275→    _supersede_dead_references(
   276→        plan,
   277→        state,
   278→        referenced_ids=referenced_ids,
   279→        now=now,
   280→        result=result,
   281→    )
   282→    _complete_empty_manual_clusters(plan, pre_sizes=pre_sizes, result=result)
   283→    _reconcile_epic_clusters(plan, state, result=result)
   284→
   285→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
   286→
   287→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   288→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   289→
   290→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   291→    if resurfaced:
   292→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   293→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   294→        # Reopen resurfaced issues in state (they were deferred)
   295→        issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   296→        for fid in resurfaced:
   297→            issue = issues.get(fid)
   298→            if issue and issue.get("status") == "deferred":
   299→                issue["status"] = "open"
   300→
   301→    # Prune old superseded entries
   302→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   303→    result.pruned = pruned
   304→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   305→
   306→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   307→    if result.changes > 0:
   308→        append_log_entry(
   309→            plan,
   310→            "reconcile",
   311→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   312→            actor="system",
   313→            detail={
   314→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   315→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   316→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   317→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   318→            },
   319→        )
   320→
   321→    return result
   322→
   323→__all__ = [
   324→    "ReconcileResult",
   325→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   326→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   327→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   328→]
   329→
```

    73→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):
    74→    """Raised when persisted scan state violates required runtime contracts."""
    75→
    76→
    77→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    78→    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
    79→    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
    80→        return
    81→    try:
    82→        config["needs_rescan"] = False
    83→        _save_config(config)
    84→    except OSError as exc:
    85→        _ = exc
    86→        config["needs_rescan"] = True
    87→
    88→
    89→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
    90→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
    91→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan_impl(runtime)
    92→
    93→
    94→def _state_subjective_assessments(
    95→    state: StateModel,
    96→) -> dict[str, object]:
    97→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
    98→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    99→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
   100→        return assessments
   101→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   102→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _ensure_state_lang_capabilities(
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   109→    """Return language capability map, creating storage when missing."""
   110→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   111→    if capabilities is None:
   112→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   113→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
   114→        return normalized
   115→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
   116→        return capabilities
   117→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   118→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
   119→    )
   120→
   121→
   122→def _state_issues(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   123→    """Return normalized issue map from state."""
   124→    issues = state.get("work_items")
   125→    if isinstance(issues, dict):
   126→        return issues
   127→    raise ScanStateContractError(
   128→        "state.issues must be an object; state file appears corrupted"
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def _subjective_reset_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   133→    """Resolve subjective dimensions that should reset on scan baseline reset."""
   134→    return resettable_default_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name)
   135→
   136→
   137→@dataclass
   138→class ScanRuntime:
   139→    """Resolved runtime context for a single scan invocation."""
   140→
   141→    args: argparse.Namespace
   142→    state_path: Path | None
   143→    state: StateModel
   144→    path: Path
   145→    config: dict[str, object]
   146→    lang: LangRun | None
   147→    lang_label: str
   148→    profile: str
   149→    effective_include_slow: bool
   150→    zone_overrides: dict[str, object] | None

    65→def _resolve_import_payload(
    66→    import_file,
    67→    *,
    68→    lang_name: str,
    69→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,
    70→) -> tuple[dict, bool, str | None]:

    82→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
    83→                override_attest=override_attest,
    84→            ),
    85→        )
    86→    except ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
    87→        print_import_load_errors(
    88→            exc.errors,
    89→            import_file=str(import_file),
    90→            colorize_fn=colorize,
    91→        )
    92→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
    93→
    94→    return issues_data, override_enabled, override_attest

    97→def _build_working_state(state: dict, state_file) -> dict:
    98→    """Return state snapshot used for import mutation/dry-run rendering."""
    99→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
   100→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
   101→        return copy.deepcopy(load_state(state_path))
   102→    return copy.deepcopy(state)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _apply_assessment_policy(
   106→    *,
   107→    working_state: dict,
   108→    issues_data: dict,
   109→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   110→) -> int:
   111→    """Apply provisional/clear flags based on assessment policy mode."""
   112→    assessment_keys = imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   113→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   114→        return mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   115→            working_state,
   116→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   117→        )
   118→    if assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   119→        clear_provisional_override_flags(
   120→            working_state,
   121→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   122→        )
   123→    return 0
   124→
   125→
   126→def _raise_on_partial_skip(diff: dict, *, allow_partial: bool) -> None:
   127→    """Refuse import when payload skips issues and partial imports are disabled."""
   128→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) <= 0 or allow_partial:
   129→        return
   130→    details_lines: list[str] = []
   131→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   132→        reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   133→        details_lines.append(
   134→            f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   135→        )
   136→    msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   137→    if details_lines:
   138→        msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   139→    msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   140→    raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   141→
   142→
   143→def _append_assessment_import_audit(
   144→    *,
   145→    working_state: dict,
   146→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   147→    provisional_count: int,
   148→    override_attest: str | None,
   149→    import_file,
   150→    import_payload: dict,
   151→) -> None:
   152→    """Record audit metadata for assessment-bearing import payloads."""
   153→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   154→        return
   155→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   156→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   157→    audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   158→    audit.append(
   159→        {
   160→            "timestamp": utc_now(),
   161→            "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   162→            "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   163→            "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   164→            "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   165→            "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   166→            "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   167→            "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   168→            "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   169→            "import_file": str(import_file),
   170→            "packet_sha256": str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
   171→        }
   172→    )
   173→
   174→
   175→def _persist_import_state(
   176→    *,
   177→    state: dict,
   178→    working_state: dict,
   179→    state_file,
   180→    diff: dict,
   181→    assessment_mode: str,
   182→    config: dict | None,
   183→    import_file: str,
   184→    import_payload: dict,
   185→) -> None:
   186→    """Persist imported state and synchronize the work plan."""
   187→    state.clear()
   188→    state.update(working_state)
   189→    save_state(state, state_file)
   190→    sync_plan_after_import(
   191→        state,
   192→        diff,
   193→        assessment_mode,
   194→        request=PlanImportSyncRequest(
   195→            state_file=state_file,
   196→            config=config,
   197→            import_file=import_file,
   198→            import_payload=import_payload,
   199→        ),
   200→    )

1→"""Shared defer-tracking helpers for triage and subjective rerun fairness."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
     9→
    10→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS = 3
    11→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS = 7
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class DeferUpdateOptions:
    16→    """Options for durable defer-state updates."""
    17→
    18→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    19→    now: str | None = None
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class DeferEscalationOptions:
    24→    """Options for defer-state escalation checks."""
    25→
    26→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    27→    now: str | None = None
    28→    min_scans: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS
    29→    min_days: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS
    30→
    31→
    32→def _coerce_non_negative_int(value: object, *, default: int = 0) -> int:
    33→    try:
    34→        parsed = int(value)
    35→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    36→        return default
    37→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else default
    38→
    39→
    40→def _scan_count(state: StateModel) -> int:
    41→    return _coerce_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count"), default=0)
    42→
    43→
    44→def _normalize_timestamp(raw: object) -> str | None:
    45→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
    46→        return None
    47→    value = raw.strip()
    48→    return value or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _parse_timestamp(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    52→    normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    53→    if not normalized:
    54→        return None
    55→    try:
    56→        return datetime.fromisoformat(normalized.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
    57→    except ValueError:
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→
    61→def _current_timestamp(
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    *,
    64→    now: str | None = None,
    65→) -> str:
    66→    for raw in (state.get("last_scan"), now):
    67→        normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    68→        if normalized:
    69→            return normalized
    70→    return utc_now()
    71→
    72→
    73→def _normalized_ids(ids: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    74→    return sorted({str(issue_id).strip() for issue_id in ids if str(issue_id).strip()})
    75→
    76→
    77→def _deferred_ids(
    78→    defer_state: dict[str, object],
    79→    *,
    80→    deferred_ids_field: str,
    81→) -> list[str]:
    82→    raw = defer_state.get(deferred_ids_field)
    83→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
    84→        return []
    85→    return sorted(
    86→        {
    87→            str(issue_id).strip()
    88→            for issue_id in raw
    89→            if str(issue_id).strip()
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def update_defer_state(
    95→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
    96→    *,
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    deferred_ids: set[str],
    99→    options: DeferUpdateOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> dict[str, object]:

## Comprehensive Postflight/Post-Scan Pipeline Analysis

Based on my thorough examination of the codebase, here's the complete postflight/post-scan pipeline:

---

### **1. POST-SCAN PIPELINE** (`desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`)

**Entry point:** `reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)` called from `ScanMergeResult` in scan workflow

#### **Phase 1: Force-Rescan Initialization** (gate: `force_rescan` parameter)
- **`_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan)`**
  - Clears ALL cycle state: synthetic items, plan_start_scores, scan_count_at_plan_start
  - Removes triage metadata (triage_recommended, etc.)
  - Prints yellow message if synthetic items removed
  - **Gate:** Only runs when `force_rescan=True`

#### **Phase 2: Issue Reconciliation** (pre-policy)
- **`_sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan, state)`** → calls `reconcile_plan_after_scan()`
  - **Only runs if plan has user content** (`queue_order`, `overrides`, `clusters`, or `skipped`)
  - Handles issue churn:
    - Supersedes dead issue references (no longer open/alive)
    - Prunes old superseded entries (>90 days TTL)
    - Completes empty manual clusters
    - Reconciles epic cluster membership
    - Resurfaces stale temporary skips (deferred→open)
    - Syncs skipped issue statuses (deferred/triaged_out)
  - Logs reconciliation with change counts
  - **Returns:** `ReconcileResult` with change count

#### **Phase 3: Policy Context Resolution** (depends on Phase 2)
- **`_subjective_policy_context(runtime, plan)`**
  - Computes `target_strict` from config
  - Computes `SubjectiveVisibility` policy (plan-aware, post-reconciliation)
  - Determines `cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`
  - **Returns:** `(target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed)` tuple

#### **Phase 4: Policy-Gated Syncs** (run sequentially with mid-cycle guard)

All of these are called within `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()` and guarded by `_is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state)`:

1. **`_sync_subjective_and_log(plan, state, policy, cycle_just_completed)`**
   - Calls `sync_subjective_dimensions()` with policy context
   - Unscored/stale/under-target dimension sync with objective backlog awareness
   - Prints: # resurfaced, # pruned, # injected
   - Logs: `"sync_subjective"` with `{"changes": True}` only if changed
   - **Gate:** Always runs

2. **`_sync_auto_clusters_and_log(plan, state, target_strict, policy)`**
   - Regenerates automatic task clusters post-merge
   - **Gate:** SKIPPED if mid-cycle OR force-rescan
   - Prints: "mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration" if deferred
   - Logs: `"auto_cluster"` with `{"changes": True}` only if changed

3. **`_sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Injects `workflow::communicate-score` when:
     - All initial reviews complete (no unscored) OR just imported trusted scores
     - Not already in queue
     - Score not already communicated this cycle (`previous_plan_start_scores` absent) unless trusted import
   - Rebaselines `plan_start_scores` to current scores when injected
   - Logs: `"sync_communicate_score"` with `{"injected": True}` only if changed
   - **Gate:** Always runs

4. **`_sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Injects `workflow::create-plan` when:
     - No unscored dimensions remain
     - Objective backlog exists
     - Not already in queue
     - No triage stages pending
   - Prints: "reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued" if injected
   - Logs: `"sync_create_plan"` with `{"injected": True}` only if changed
   - **Gate:** Always runs

5. **`_sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Calls `sync_triage_needed()` with policy (enables mid-cycle defer guard)
   - Injects triage stage IDs when review issues change since last triage
   - **Deferred result:** Prints "review work items changed — triage recommended" (dim), returns False early
   - **Injected result:** Prints "planning mode needed — review work items changed since last triage" (cyan)
   - Logs: `"sync_triage"` with `{"injected": True}` only if not deferred
   - **Gate:** Always runs (but may be deferred)

6. **Plan-start Score Sync** (gate: `NOT force_rescan`)
   - **`_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state)`** calls:
     - `_seed_plan_start_scores()` if empty or reset sentinel
       - Captures strict/overall/objective/verified scores
       - Clears previous_plan_start_scores sentinel (cycle start)
       - Records scan_count_at_plan_start
       - Logs: `"seed_start_scores"`
     - `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty()` if scores exist and queue drains
       - **Guards:** Don't clear while communicate-score pending (was just injected)
       - Clears scores once queue fully drained
       - Logs: `"clear_start_scores"`

7. **Postflight Scan Completion** (gate: `NOT force_rescan`)
   - **`_sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state)`** → `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready()`
     - **Guards:**
       - NOT if deferred disposition work remains (`build_deferred_disposition_item()` is not None)
       - NOT if objective backlog remains (checked via `_has_objective_cycle()`)
     - Sets `refresh_state["postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count"] = scan_count`
     - Logs: `"complete_postflight_scan"` with `{"scan_count": int}`

8. **Lifecycle Phase Sync**
   - **`_sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state)`**
     - Resolves phase from `queue_context().snapshot.phase` (work queue computation)
     - Normalizes to coarse phase name: SCAN, REVIEW, WORKFLOW, TRIAGE, EXECUTE
     - Logs: `"sync_lifecycle_phase"` with `{"phase": phase_name}` if changed
     - **Gate:** Always runs

#### **Phase 5: Persistence**
- **`save_plan(plan, plan_path)`** only if dirty flag is set
- Catches PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS and logs warning without re-raising

---

### **2. POST-IMPORT PIPELINE** (`desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`)

**Entry point:** `sync_plan_after_import(state, diff, assessment_mode, request=...)`
Called from `_persist_import_state()` in review/importing/cmd.py after state is saved

#### **Phase 1: Setup & Precondition Checks**
- **Load plan:** `load_plan()` from `plan_path_for_state()` or skip if no living plan
- **Compute policy:** `compute_subjective_visibility(state, target_strict, plan)`
- **Compute current scores:** For rebaselining (`ScoreSnapshot` from state)
- **Determine trust mode:** `trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}`
- **Exit early if no changes needed**

#### **Phase 2: Workflow Gate Syncs** (3 parallel calls)

1. **`sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy, scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import, current_scores)`**
   - Re-baselines scores immediately if trusted import
   - Sets `previous_plan_start_scores` sentinel
   - Records in `mutations.workflow_injected_ids`
   - Logs appended later via `_append_workflow_log_entries()`

2. **`sync_import_scores_needed(plan, state, assessment_mode, import_file, import_payload)`**
   - Injects `workflow::import-scores` ONLY if `assessment_mode == "issues_only"`
   - Checks stale pending import metadata
   - Records pending meta in `refresh_state["pending_import_scores"]`
   - Logs appended later

3. **`sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy)`**
   - Same gate logic as post-scan version
   - Logs appended later

#### **Phase 3: Review Delta Sync**
- **`_sync_review_delta(mutations, plan, state, policy, sync_inputs)`**
  - **Gate:** Only runs if `sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta` (new/reopened/auto_resolved > 0)
  - Calls **`sync_plan_after_review_import(plan, state, policy)`** (pure engine function):
    - Prunes stale review IDs from queue (auto-resolved issues)
    - Adds new review issue IDs to `queue_order`
    - Calls `sync_triage_needed()` to inject triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard)
    - **Returns:** `ReviewImportSyncResult` with:
      - `new_ids`: issues added to queue
      - `added_to_queue`: subset actually queued
      - `stale_pruned_from_queue`: auto-resolved IDs removed
      - `triage_injected`: whether stages were injected
      - `triage_deferred`: whether triage was deferred (backlog pending)
      - `triage_injected_ids`: actual triage stage IDs added
  - Sets `mutations.import_result` and marks dirty
  - **Prints** via `_print_review_import_sync()`:
    - New review items (up to 10 with `short_issue_id`, "... and N more")
    - Stale review prunes
    - Footer with next-step instructions (plan queue, backlog, `desloppify next`, triage commands)

#### **Phase 4: Subjective Dimension Rerun Sync**
- **`_sync_subjective_queue_after_import(mutations, plan, state, policy, target_strict, sync_inputs)`**
  - **Gate:** Only runs if `has_review_issue_delta` OR assessment_keys present
  - Calls `sync_subjective_dimensions()` (same as post-scan)
    - Uses `cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`
  - **Then immediately auto-clusters** via `auto_cluster_issues(plan, state, target_strict, policy)`
  - Sets `mutations.stale_sync_result` and `mutations.auto_cluster_changes`
  - Marks dirty if either changed

#### **Phase 5: Lifecycle Phase Sync**
- **`_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan, state, policy)`**
  - **Gate:** Only if mutations are dirty (i.e., something changed)
  - Computes full lifecycle context:
    - `has_initial_reviews = bool(policy.unscored_ids)`
    - `has_objective_backlog = bool(policy.has_objective_backlog)`
    - `has_postflight_review`: checks for triage findings + assessment requests in state OR stale/under-target in policy
    - `has_postflight_workflow`: checks for import-scores, communicate-score, score-checkpoint, create-plan in queue
    - `has_triage`: checks for triage stage IDs in queue
    - `has_deferred`: checks for temporary skip entries in plan.skipped
  - Calls `sync_lifecycle_phase()` to resolve and persist

#### **Phase 6: Logging**
- **`_append_workflow_log_entries()`** — logs three workflow syncs
  - `sync_communicate_score`: `{"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True}`
  - `sync_import_scores`: `{"trigger": "review_import", "injected": bool, "pruned": list}`
  - `sync_create_plan`: `{"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True}`

- **`_append_review_import_sync_log()`** — comprehensive import log entry with:
  - `trigger: "review_import"`
  - `new_ids`: list of new review IDs
  - `added_to_queue`: subset queued
  - `workflow_injected_ids`: list of workflow IDs injected
  - `triage_injected` / `triage_injected_ids` / `triage_deferred`
  - `diff_new`, `diff_reopened`, `diff_auto_resolved`
  - `stale_pruned_from_queue`
  - `covered_subjective`: assessment keys covered
  - `stale_sync_injected` / `stale_sync_pruned`
  - `auto_cluster_changes`

#### **Phase 7: Persistence & Display**
- **Save plan** only if mutations.dirty
- **Print summary** via `_print_review_import_sync()` if import_result is not None
- **Print workflow injected message** if workflow IDs were added

---

### **3. RECONCILE REVIEW IMPORT** (engine layer)

**File:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py`

**Pure engine function:** `sync_plan_after_review_import(plan, state, policy=None)` → `ReviewImportSyncResult | None`

**Logic:**
1. Prunes stale review IDs (auto-resolved)
2. Determines IDs to add:
   - If triage baseline exists: only NEW IDs (post-triage)
   - If no baseline yet: ALL open review IDs (first import cannot drop work)
3. Appends new IDs to `queue_order`
4. Injects triage stages via `sync_triage_needed()` if work changed
5. Returns None if no changes, else `ReviewImportSyncResult`

---

### **4. EXISTING SYNC/ PACKAGE** (`desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/`)

**Current modules:**

| Module | Purpose |
|--------|---------|
| `context.py` | `is_mid_cycle()` and `has_objective_backlog()` — shared predicates |
| `triage.py` | `sync_triage_needed()` — inject/prune triage stages, mid-cycle defer guard, escalation |
| `dimensions.py` | `sync_subjective_dimensions()` — unified unscored/stale/under-target sync with objective backlog awareness |
| `workflow_gates.py` | Re-exports from workflow.py (app-facing stable API) |
| `workflow.py` | `sync_communicate_score_needed()`, `sync_create_plan_needed()`, `sync_import_scores_needed()` |
| `defer_policy.py` | Shared defer-state tracking and escalation helpers (triage + subjective) |
| `triage_start_policy.py` | `decide_triage_start()` — triage defer decision logic |
| `auto_prune.py` | Auto-cluster stale pruning helper |

---

### **KEY NUANCES & GATES**

#### **Mid-Cycle vs. Cycle Boundary**
- **Mid-cycle:** `plan_start_scores` is set (real values), queue has items
  - **Skips:** auto-clustering, triage injection, unscored injection, postflight scan completion
  - **Still runs:** subjective sync, communicate-score, create-plan, lifecycle phase
  - **Triage:** Deferred (banner shown, metadata updated) unless escalated

#### **Force-Rescan**
- Clears synthetic items, plan_start_scores, triage metadata
- Skips plan-start seeding and postflight completion
- Auto-clustering still deferred (checked via mid-cycle guard)

#### **Triage Deferral Logic**
- **Defer when:** objective backlog exists AND new review issues appeared
- **Escalate when:** same issues deferred for 3+ scans AND 7+ days
- **On escalation:** force-inject triage stages even with backlog, normalize prefixes

#### **Lifecycle Phase Resolution** (in order)
1. Has initial reviews (unscored) → REVIEW
2. Has objective backlog → EXECUTE
3. Has deferred items OR postflight scan pending → SCAN
4. Has postflight review work → REVIEW
5. Has postflight workflow items → WORKFLOW
6. Has triage stages → TRIAGE
7. Fall back to persisted or SCAN

#### **Subjective Dimension Sync**
- **Unscored:** Only injected at cycle boundaries (not mid-cycle)
- **Stale/under-target:** Injected if no backlog, evicted if backlog + not escalated, force-promoted if escalated

#### **Postflight Scan Completion Guard**
- Requires: queue empty AND no deferred disposition AND no objective backlog
- Sets `refresh_state["postflight_scan_completed_at_scan_count"]`
- Cleared by objective work resumption or lifecycle reset

#### **Logging Entry Points**
- `append_log_entry(plan, actor="system", detail={...})` called from multiple sync helpers
- Each log entry records trigger, what was injected/pruned/deferred
- Review import has comprehensive log capturing all delta details

---

### **SUMMARY TABLE: Post-Scan vs. Post-Import**

| Step | Post-Scan | Post-Import | Notes |
|------|-----------|------------|-------|
| Force-rescan cycle reset | Yes | N/A | Only post-scan |
| Issue reconciliation (dead refs) | Yes | No | Only post-scan |
| Policy computation | Yes | Yes | Post-import: always, Post-scan: after reconcile |
| Subjective dimension sync | Yes | Yes | Same function, slightly different cycle context |
| Auto-clustering | Yes (mid-cycle gated) | Yes (post-import always) | Post-import always runs |
| Triage injection | Yes | Yes | Both respect mid-cycle defer |
| Workflow gates | Yes (3 gates) | Yes (3 gates) | Identical gate logic |
| Lifecycle phase sync | Yes | Yes (if dirty) | Post-import conditional on changes |
| Plan-start score seed/clear | Yes | No | Only post-scan |
| Postflight scan completion | Yes (if ready) | No | Only post-scan |
| Logging | Yes | Yes | Different entry points, Post-import more detailed |

---

### **FILES TO VERIFY UNIFICATION PLAN**

For your proposed unification, verify that a merged function captures:

1. **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` lines 541–579:** Entire `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` orchestration
2. **`desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py` lines 380–500:** Entire `sync_plan_after_import()` orchestration
3. **`desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py` lines 148–199:** Pure engine `sync_plan_after_review_import()` 
4. **`desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/` package:** All sync modules (context, triage, dimensions, workflow, defer_policy)
5. **`desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py` lines 106–156:** `sync_lifecycle_phase()` and phase resolution

The pipeline has 7–8 distinct orchestration steps that differ slightly between contexts (mid-cycle guards, conditional execution, optional lifecycle resolution). A unification should preserve these conditionals as explicit parameters rather than hiding them in shared code logic.

Good, I have a thorough picture of both pipelines. Here's my analysis of the plan:

---

## Overall Verdict: Solid plan, a few gaps worth addressing

The plan correctly identifies the core problem (duplicated gate orchestration) and the right solution (single engine-level pipeline). The incremental implementation order is correct. Here are the specific issues I found:

### Gaps / Missing Items

**1. Auto-clustering gate asymmetry is under-specified**

The plan says post-scan skips auto-clustering when `mid_cycle`, but in the current post-import path, auto-clustering **always runs** (no mid-cycle guard). The pipeline uses `skip_auto_cluster` and scan passes `mid_cycle`, but the plan for the import caller (Step 4.6) passes `skip_auto_cluster=not has_data_change`. This is a subtle behavior change from the current import path — today, import always clusters when there's data. Confirm this is intentional or at least note it as a known delta.

**2. Lifecycle phase resolution diverges between the two paths — plan doesn't fully address this**

The current post-scan lifecycle uses `queue_context().snapshot.phase` (work-queue-derived), but the current post-import lifecycle uses a **hand-rolled resolution** with explicit `has_initial_reviews`, `has_objective_backlog`, `has_postflight_review`, `has_postflight_workflow`, `has_triage`, `has_deferred` flags (`_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import`). The plan says to unify on `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase` and calls it an "intentional behavior change" — good that it's acknowledged. But the plan doesn't enumerate **what changes** this causes. For example:
- The import path currently checks `has_deferred` (temporary skips) for SCAN phase — does `queue_context` replicate this?
- The import path checks `has_postflight_review` which includes assessment requests in state — does snapshot.phase capture this?

You should add a test case or comment that explicitly documents the expected behavioral deltas from this unification.

**3. `_sync_review_delta` currently calls `sync_plan_after_review_import` which internally calls `sync_triage_needed` — the plan handles this correctly with `inject_triage=False`, but misses the gate on review delta existence**

In Step 4.3, the plan says "run `_sync_review_delta(...)` first, with triage disabled" — but the current code gates this on `sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta`. The plan should be explicit that the existing gate stays (the plan does mention it in Step 4.4 for `has_data_change`, but the delta sync gate itself should be stated).

**4. Force-rescan cycle reset is unmentioned**

The plan says scan-specific helpers stay in place, but `_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan` isn't listed in either the "delete" or "keep" lists in Step 3. It should be explicitly listed under "keep" since it's scan-specific pre-pipeline logic.

**5. Issue reconciliation (`_sync_post_scan_without_policy`) is unmentioned**

Similarly, the pre-policy reconciliation step (`reconcile_plan_after_scan`) that handles dead refs, superseded entries, stale skips, etc. isn't mentioned in the plan. It's scan-specific and should stay, but it should be listed under "keep" for completeness.

**6. Post-import conditional lifecycle gate ("only if dirty") might change**

Currently post-import only syncs lifecycle if mutations are dirty. With the pipeline always running lifecycle as Step 7, lifecycle will now always be computed even when nothing changed. This is minor but could cause spurious log entries for `sync_lifecycle_phase` with no actual phase change. Consider adding a "changed" guard inside the pipeline.

**7. Missing: `_record_workflow_change` integration details**

Step 4 says `_merge_pipeline_workflow_results` feeds results through `_record_workflow_change`, but the plan doesn't specify how `PostflightResult` fields map to the existing `_PlanImportMutations` structure. Specifically: where do `workflow_injected_ids` come from? Currently they're collected from individual workflow gate calls. The plan should state that `PostflightResult` exposes an aggregate `workflow_injected_ids` property or that the merge helper extracts them.

### Minor Nits

- **Step 2 pipeline order:** `sync_import_scores_needed` (Step 4) takes `import_file` and `import_payload` — these come from `PostflightContext`. But post-scan never has these. The plan should note that Step 4 is naturally a no-op when these context fields are None.
- **`_has_postflight_review_work` appears in both delete lists** (Step 3 and Step 4). Different functions with the same name in different files — fine, but worth a comment for the implementer.
- **Test plan doesn't mention testing force-rescan + pipeline interaction** — force-rescan clears state, then pipeline runs. Worth a test.

### What the Plan Gets Right

- Triage ownership moving to the pipeline, with `inject_triage=False` on the engine call — this correctly fixes the `create-plan` ordering bug
- `target_strict` always passed explicitly — prevents subtle policy drift
- Plan-start scores and postflight-scan staying scan-specific — correct separation
- Pipeline staying engine-only with no app imports
- Incremental implementation order with engine changes first

### Recommended Additions to the Plan

1. Add `_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan` and `_sync_post_scan_without_policy`/`reconcile_plan_after_scan` to the "keep" list in Step 3
2. Add a section documenting expected behavioral deltas from lifecycle unification
3. Add a `workflow_injected_ids` property or aggregation note to `PostflightResult`
4. Add test cases for: force-rescan + pipeline, lifecycle phase delta verification, "no changes = no spurious logs"
5. Note that `import_scores` gate is naturally a no-op for scan callers (context fields None)

   102→Keep scan-specific helpers in place:
   103→

   105→- `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty`

   129→
   130→Rewrite `_sync_post_scan_with_policy(...)` to:
   131→
   132→- compute `mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan`
   133→- build `PostflightContext(current_scores=ScoreSnapshot(...))`
   134→- call `run_postflight_gates(...)` with:

   145→### 4. Refactor review import sync to call pipeline
   146→
   147→In `desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`:
   148→
   149→Delete:

   176→- It should copy `result.subjective` and `result.auto_cluster_changes` into `mutations` for structured logging

   180→

   184→4. compute `has_data_change = sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta or bool(sync_inputs.assessment_keys)`

   188→   - `target_strict=target_strict`
   189→   - `context=ctx`

   191→   - `skip_auto_cluster=not has_data_change`

   199→- `workflow_injected_ids` in that log should now come from pipeline results merged into `mutations`.
   200→- `stale_sync_injected`, `stale_sync_pruned`, and `auto_cluster_changes` should continue to reflect subjective/cluster work when the pipeline ran.
   201→- If `inject_triage=False`, `mutations.import_result` should naturally show no triage injection; triage state now comes from the pipeline and should not be duplicated into `ReviewImportSyncResult`.
   202→
   203→### 5. Update package surface and imports
   204→
   205→Update imports to use the new pipeline module and public exports from `engine._plan.sync`.
   206→
   207→Do not keep duplicate local wrappers that only forward to the pipeline.
   208→
   209→## Test Plan
   210→
   211→### New tests
   212→
   213→Add `desloppify/tests/plan/test_postflight_pipeline.py` covering:
   214→
   215→- pipeline runs gates in the required order
   216→- `create-plan` can inject before triage when review delta ran first with `inject_triage=False`
   217→- `skip_subjective=True` prevents subjective queue mutations
   218→- `skip_auto_cluster=True` prevents cluster regeneration
   219→- import-scores gate only runs when `assessment_mode` is provided
   220→- lifecycle is persisted from `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase`
   221→- unified lifecycle behavior:
   222→  - initial-review phase only appears at a fresh boundary
   223→  - mid-cycle unscored dimensions do not force lifecycle back to `review`
   224→- second pipeline run is a no-op in terms of additional mutations
   225→- pipeline does not seed/clear `plan_start_scores`
   226→- pipeline does not mark postflight scan complete
   227→
   228→### Existing tests to update
   229→
   230→Update direct scan/import tests to match the new ownership split:
   231→
   232→- scan tests that import moved helpers should target the pipeline or retained scan-specific helpers
   233→- review-import tests should stop assuming `sync_plan_after_review_import()` injects triage in the app flow
   234→- source-contract tests should assert the new shape:
   235→  - import path calls `_sync_review_delta(...)`
   236→  - import path calls `run_postflight_gates(...)`
   237→  - workflow gate orchestration no longer appears inline in `sync_plan_after_import(...)`
   238→- keep engine direct tests for `sync_plan_after_review_import()` unchanged where possible, since default `inject_triage=True` preserves old behavior
   239→
   240→### Verification commands
   241→
   242→Run:
   243→
   244→1. `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q`
   245→2. `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/commands/test_lifecycle_transitions.py -v`
   246→3. `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/engine/test_sync_split_modules_direct.py -v`

   248→5. `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/commands/scan/ -v`
   249→6. `python -m desloppify --lang python scan --path desloppify`
   250→
   251→## Assumptions And Defaults
   252→
   253→- Lifecycle unification on snapshot phase is intentional and accepted as a behavior change.
   254→- `sync_plan_after_review_import(..., inject_triage=False)` is only for orchestrator use; engine direct callers keep default behavior.
   255→- The pipeline is postflight-only. Scan-specific plan-start-score and postflight-scan completion logic remain outside it.
   256→- No gate predicate or queue mutation semantics change beyond triage ownership and lifecycle unification.
   257→- Incremental implementation order:
   258→  1. make review-import triage optional
   259→  2. add pipeline module and exports
   260→  3. refactor scan caller
   261→  4. refactor import caller
   262→  5. update/add tests

  "old_string": "## Implementation Changes",
  "new_string": "## Known Behavioral Deltas\n\nThis unification intentionally changes some behaviors. These must be tested explicitly.\n\n### Lifecycle phase resolution\n\nThe current post-import path (`_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import`) uses a hand-rolled resolution with explicit flags: `has_initial_reviews`, `has_objective_backlog`, `has_postflight_review`, `has_postflight_workflow`, `has_triage`, `has_deferred`. The unified pipeline replaces this with `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase`.\n\nExpected deltas to verify:\n\n- **Deferred/temporary skips:** The import path currently checks `has_deferred` (temporary skip entries in `plan.skipped`) to resolve SCAN phase. Confirm `queue_context` snapshot replicates this or document the acceptable change.\n- **Assessment requests in state:** The import path checks `has_postflight_review` which includes assessment requests in state (not just policy unscored/stale). Confirm snapshot.phase accounts for this or accept the narrowing.\n- **Conditional execution:** The import path only syncs lifecycle when mutations are dirty. The pipeline always runs lifecycle (Step 7). Guard inside the pipeline: only persist and set `lifecycle_phase_changed=True` when the phase actually differs from the current persisted value, to avoid spurious log entries.\n\n### Auto-clustering gate asymmetry\n\nThe current post-import path **always** runs auto-clustering when there's a data change (no mid-cycle guard). The pipeline uses `skip_auto_cluster`, and the import caller passes `skip_auto_cluster=not has_data_change`. This preserves the \"only cluster when data changed\" behavior but is worth noting as a formalization — previously it was implicit via the conditional call site.\n\n## Implementation Changes"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's what I added/changed:

1. **New "Known Behavioral Deltas" section** before Implementation Changes — documents the lifecycle resolution divergence (hand-rolled flags vs. snapshot.phase), the specific things to verify (deferred skips, assessment requests, conditional execution), and the auto-clustering gate asymmetry.

2. **Expanded "Keep" list in Step 3** — added `_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan`, `_sync_post_scan_without_policy`/`reconcile_plan_after_scan`, and `_is_mid_cycle_scan` which were previously unlisted.

3. **`workflow_injected_ids` property** added to `PostflightResult` spec — aggregates IDs from the three workflow gates for structured logging.

4. **Pipeline Step 4/7 notes** — clarified that `import_scores` is naturally a no-op for scan callers, and that lifecycle persistence should guard on actual phase change to avoid spurious logs.

5. **Explicit review-delta gate** in Step 4.3 — noted that the existing `has_review_issue_delta` gate is preserved.

6. **Expanded test plan** — added cases for: force-rescan + pipeline interaction, lifecycle no-op when unchanged, lifecycle behavioral delta verification (deferred skips, assessment requests), and `workflow_injected_ids` aggregation.

    48→
    49→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    50→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    51→
    52→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    53→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    54→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    55→    """
    56→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    57→    synthetic = [item for item in order if is_synthetic_id(item)]
    58→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    59→        return False
    60→    for item in synthetic:
    61→        order.remove(item)
    62→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    63→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    64→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    65→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    66→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    67→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    68→    count = len(synthetic)
    69→    if count:
    70→        print(
    71→            colorize(
    72→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    73→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    74→                "yellow",
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→    return True
    78→
    79→
    80→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    81→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    82→    return bool(
    83→        plan.get("queue_order")
    84→        or plan.get("overrides")
    85→        or plan.get("clusters")
    86→        or plan.get("skipped")
    87→    )
    88→
    89→
    90→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    91→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    92→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    93→        return False
    94→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    95→    if recon.resurfaced:
    96→        print(
    97→            colorize(
    98→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    99→                "cyan",
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→    return bool(recon.changes)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _sync_subjective_dimensions_display(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   106→    """Sync all subjective dimensions (unscored + stale + under-target) in plan queue."""
   107→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   108→    if sync.resurfaced:
   109→        print(
   110→            colorize(
   111→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.resurfaced)} skipped subjective dimension(s) resurfaced — never reviewed.",
   112→                "yellow",
   113→            )
   114→        )
   115→    if sync.pruned:
   116→        print(
   117→            colorize(
   118→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   119→                "cyan",
   120→            )
   121→        )
   122→    if sync.injected:
   123→        print(
   124→            colorize(
   125→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   126→                "cyan",
   127→            )
   128→        )
   129→    return bool(sync.changes)

   131→
   132→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   133→    plan: dict[str, object],
   134→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   135→    *,
   136→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   137→    policy=None,
   138→) -> bool:
   139→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   140→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   141→        plan, state,
   142→        target_strict=target_strict,
   143→        policy=policy,
   144→    ))
   145→
   146→
   147→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   148→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   149→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   150→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   151→        return False
   152→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   153→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   154→        return False
   155→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   156→    if scores.strict is None:
   157→        return False
   158→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   159→        "strict": scores.strict,
   160→        "overall": scores.overall,
   161→        "objective": scores.objective,
   162→        "verified": scores.verified,
   163→    }
   164→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   165→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   166→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   167→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   168→    return True
   169→
   170→
   171→def _has_objective_cycle(
   172→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   173→    plan: dict[str, object],
   174→) -> bool | None:
   175→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   176→    try:
   177→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   178→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   182→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   183→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   184→        return None
   185→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   186→
   187→
   188→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   189→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   190→) -> bool:
   191→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   192→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   193→        return False
   194→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   195→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   196→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   197→        return False
   198→
   199→    try:
   200→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   201→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   202→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   203→            score_display_mode,
   204→        )
   205→
   206→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   207→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   208→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   209→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   210→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   211→        return False
   212→    if not queue_empty:
   213→        return False
   214→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   215→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   216→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   217→    # in the next cycle.
   218→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   219→    return True
   220→
   221→
   222→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   223→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   224→    plan: dict[str, object],
   225→) -> bool:
   226→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   227→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   228→        return False
   229→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   230→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   231→        return False
   232→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   233→        plan,
   234→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   235→    )
   236→

   238→def _subjective_policy_context(
   239→    runtime: Any,
   240→    plan: dict[str, object],
   241→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   242→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   243→
   244→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   245→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   246→        runtime.state,
   247→        target_strict=target_strict,
   248→        plan=plan,
   249→    )
   250→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   251→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed

   253→
   254→def _sync_subjective_and_log(
   255→    plan: dict[str, object],
   256→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   257→    *,
   258→    policy,
   259→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   260→) -> bool:
   261→    changed = _sync_subjective_dimensions_display(
   262→        plan,
   263→        state,
   264→        lambda p, s: sync_subjective_dimensions(
   265→            p,
   266→            s,
   267→            policy=policy,
   268→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   269→        ),
   270→    )
   271→    if changed:
   272→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_subjective", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   273→    return changed

   275→
   276→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   277→    plan: dict[str, object],
   278→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   279→    *,
   280→    target_strict: float,
   281→    policy,
   282→) -> bool:
   283→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   284→        plan,
   285→        state,
   286→        target_strict=target_strict,
   287→        policy=policy,
   288→    )
   289→    if changed:
   290→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   291→    return changed

   293→
   294→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   295→    plan: dict[str, object],
   296→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   297→    *,
   298→    policy=None,
   299→) -> bool:
   300→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   301→    if triage_sync.deferred:
   302→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   303→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   304→            print(
   305→                colorize(
   306→                    "  Plan: review work items changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   307→                    "dim",
   308→                )
   309→            )
   310→        return False
   311→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   312→        return False
   313→    if triage_sync.injected:
   314→        print(
   315→            colorize(
   316→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review work items changed since last triage.",
   317→                "cyan",
   318→            )
   319→        )
   320→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   321→    return True
   322→
   323→
   324→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   325→    plan: dict[str, object],
   326→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   327→    *,
   328→    policy,
   329→) -> bool:
   330→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   331→    current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   332→        strict=snapshot.strict,
   333→        overall=snapshot.overall,
   334→        objective=snapshot.objective,
   335→        verified=snapshot.verified,
   336→    )
   337→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   338→        plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores,
   339→    )
   340→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   341→        return False
   342→    append_log_entry(
   343→        plan,
   344→        "sync_communicate_score",
   345→        actor="system",
   346→        detail={"injected": True},
   347→    )
   348→    return True
   349→
   350→
   351→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   352→    plan: dict[str, object],
   353→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   354→    *,
   355→    policy,
   356→) -> bool:
   357→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   358→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   359→        return False
   360→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   361→        print(
   362→            colorize(
   363→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",

   365→            )
   366→        )
   367→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})

   370→
   371→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   372→    plan: dict[str, object],
   373→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   374→) -> bool:
   375→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   376→    if seeded:
   377→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   378→        return True
   379→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   380→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   381→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   382→    if cleared:
   383→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   384→    return cleared
   385→
   386→
   387→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   388→    plan: dict[str, object],
   389→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   390→) -> bool:
   391→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   392→    if changed:
   393→        append_log_entry(
   394→            plan,
   395→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   396→            actor="system",
   397→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
