```
   401→
   402→def _has_postflight_review_work(
   403→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   404→    *,
   405→    policy,
   406→) -> bool:
   407→    issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   408→    has_review_like_issue = any(
   409→        isinstance(issue, dict)
   410→        and issue.get("status") == "open"
   411→        and (is_triage_finding(issue) or is_assessment_request(issue))
   412→        for issue in issues.values()
   413→    )
   414→    if has_review_like_issue:
   415→        return True
   416→    return bool(policy.stale_ids or policy.under_target_ids)
   417→
   418→
   419→def _has_postflight_workflow_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   420→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   421→    return any(
   422→        item_id in order
   423→        for item_id in (
   424→            "workflow::import-scores",
   425→            "workflow::communicate-score",
   426→            "workflow::score-checkpoint",
   427→            "workflow::create-plan",
   428→        )
   429→    )
   430→
   431→
   432→def _has_triage_items(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
   433→    return any(
   434→        isinstance(item_id, str) and is_triage_id(item_id)
   435→        for item_id in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(
   440→    plan: dict[str, object],
   441→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   442→) -> bool:
   443→    phase = coarse_phase_name(queue_context(state, plan=plan).snapshot.phase)
   444→    changed = set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
   445→    if changed:
   446→        append_log_entry(
   447→            plan,
   448→            "sync_lifecycle_phase",
   449→            actor="system",
   450→            detail={"phase": phase},
   451→        )
   452→    return changed
   453→
   454→
   455→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   456→    *,
   457→    plan: dict[str, object],
   458→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   459→) -> bool:
   460→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   461→    return bool(_apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan))
   462→
   463→
   464→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   465→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   466→
   467→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   468→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   469→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   470→
   471→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   472→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   473→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   474→    """
   475→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   476→        return False
   477→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   478→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   479→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   480→
   481→
   482→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   483→    *,
   484→    plan: dict[str, object],
   485→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   486→    target_strict: float,
   487→    policy,
   488→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   489→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   490→) -> bool:
   491→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   492→
   493→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   494→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   495→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   496→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   497→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   498→    """
   499→    dirty = False
   500→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   501→
   502→    if _sync_subjective_and_log(
   503→        plan,
   504→        state,
   505→        policy=policy,
   506→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   507→    ):
   508→        dirty = True
   509→    if not mid_cycle:
   510→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   511→            plan,
   512→            state,
   513→            target_strict=target_strict,
   514→            policy=policy,
   515→        ):
   516→            dirty = True
   517→    else:
   518→        print(
   519→            colorize(
   520→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   521→                "preserve queue state.",
   522→                "dim",
   523→            )
   524→        )
   525→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   526→        dirty = True
   527→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   528→        dirty = True
   529→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   530→        dirty = True
   531→    if not force_rescan:
   532→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   533→            dirty = True
   534→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   535→            dirty = True
   536→    if _sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log(plan, state):
   537→        dirty = True
   538→    return dirty
   539→
   540→
   541→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: Any) -> None:
   542→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   543→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   544→    try:
   545→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   546→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   547→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   548→        return
   549→
   550→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   551→
   552→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   553→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   554→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   555→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   556→
   557→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   558→
   559→    # Policy must be computed after reconciliation, which mutates plan
   560→    # (supersede/prune resolved issues) before policy reads it.
   561→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   562→        runtime,
   563→        plan,
   564→    )
   565→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   566→        plan=plan,
   567→        state=runtime.state,
   568→        target_strict=target_strict,
   569→        policy=policy,
   570→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   571→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   572→    ) or dirty
   573→
   574→    if dirty:
   575→        try:
   576→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   577→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   578→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   579→
```
```

   120→    )[1]
   121→
   122→
   123→def _print_review_import_sync(
   124→    state: dict,
   125→    result: ReviewImportSyncResult,
   126→    *,
   127→    workflow_injected: bool,
   128→) -> None:
   129→    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
   130→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   131→    stale_pruned = result.stale_pruned_from_queue
   132→    print()
   133→    _print_new_review_items(state, new_ids)
   134→    _print_stale_review_prunes(stale_pruned)
   135→    _print_review_import_footer(result, workflow_injected=workflow_injected)
   136→
   137→
   138→def _print_new_review_items(state: dict, new_ids: list[str]) -> None:
   139→    if not new_ids:
   140→        return
   141→    print(colorize(
   142→        f"  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review work item(s) added to queue.",
   143→        "bold",
   144→    ))
   145→    issues = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {}))
   146→    for finding_id in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
   147→        finding = issues.get(finding_id, {})
   148→        print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(finding_id)}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
   149→    if len(new_ids) > 10:
   150→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
   151→
   152→
   153→def _print_stale_review_prunes(stale_pruned: list[str]) -> None:
   154→    if not stale_pruned:
   155→        return
   156→    print(colorize(
   157→        f"  Plan updated: {len(stale_pruned)} stale review work item(s) removed from queue.",
   158→        "bold",
   159→    ))
   160→
   161→
   162→def _print_review_import_footer(
   163→    result: ReviewImportSyncResult,
   164→    *,
   165→    workflow_injected: bool,
   166→) -> None:
   167→    print()
   168→    print(colorize(

   172→    print()
   173→    print(colorize("  View execution queue:  desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
   174→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   175→    print(colorize("  View broader backlog:  desloppify backlog", "dim"))
   176→    print()
   177→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:", "yellow"))
   178→    print(colorize("    Run:    desloppify next", "yellow"))
   179→    if result.triage_injected and not workflow_injected:

   181→        print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
   182→        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
   183→    print(colorize(
   184→        "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",
   185→        "dim",

   188→
   189→def _review_delta_present(diff: dict) -> bool:
   190→    return any(
   191→        int(diff.get(key, 0) or 0) > 0
   192→        for key in ("new", "reopened", "auto_resolved")
   193→    )
   194→
   195→
   196→def _print_workflow_injected_message(workflow_injected_ids: list[str]) -> None:
   197→    if not workflow_injected_ids:
   198→        return
   199→    injected_parts = [f"`{workflow_id}`" for workflow_id in workflow_injected_ids]
   200→    print(colorize(
   201→        f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   202→        "cyan",
   203→    ))

   205→
   206→def _build_import_sync_inputs(
   207→    diff: dict,
   208→    import_payload: NormalizedReviewImportPayload | None,
   209→) -> _ImportSyncInputs:
   210→    assessment_keys = (
   211→        imported_assessment_keys(import_payload)
   212→        if isinstance(import_payload, dict)
   213→        else set()
   214→    )
   215→    return _ImportSyncInputs(
   216→        has_review_issue_delta=_review_delta_present(diff),
   217→        assessment_keys=frozenset(assessment_keys),
   218→        covered_ids=tuple(
   219→            f"subjective::{dim_key}"
   220→            for dim_key in sorted(assessment_keys)
   221→        ),
   222→    )
   223→
   224→
   225→def _record_workflow_change(
   226→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   227→    result,
   228→    *,
   229→    workflow_id: str,
   230→    injected: bool = True,
   231→) -> None:
   232→    if not result.changes:
   233→        return
   234→    mutations.dirty = True
   235→    if injected:
   236→        mutations.workflow_injected_ids.append(workflow_id)
   237→
   238→
   239→def _sync_review_delta(
   240→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   241→    plan: dict,
   242→    state: dict,
   243→    *,
   244→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
   245→    sync_inputs: _ImportSyncInputs,
   246→) -> None:
   247→    if not sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta:
   248→        return
   249→    mutations.import_result = sync_plan_after_review_import(
   250→        plan,
   251→        state,
   252→        policy=policy,
   253→    )
   254→    if mutations.import_result is not None:
   255→        mutations.dirty = True
   256→
   257→
   258→def _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   259→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   260→    plan: dict,
   261→    state: dict,
   262→    *,
   263→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility,
   264→    target_strict: float,
   265→    sync_inputs: _ImportSyncInputs,
   266→) -> None:
   267→    if not (sync_inputs.has_review_issue_delta or sync_inputs.assessment_keys):
   268→        return
   269→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   270→    mutations.stale_sync_result = sync_subjective_dimensions(
   271→        plan,
   272→        state,
   273→        policy=policy,
   274→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   275→    )
   276→    if mutations.stale_sync_result.changes:
   277→        mutations.dirty = True
   278→
   279→    mutations.auto_cluster_changes = int(auto_cluster_issues(
   280→        plan,
   281→        state,
   282→        target_strict=target_strict,
   283→        policy=policy,
   284→    ))
   285→    if mutations.auto_cluster_changes:
   286→        mutations.dirty = True
   287→
   288→
   289→def _append_workflow_log_entries(
   290→    plan: dict,
   291→    *,
   292→    communicate_result,
   293→    import_scores_result,
   294→    create_plan_result,
   295→) -> None:
   296→    if communicate_result.changes:
   297→        append_log_entry(
   298→            plan,
   299→            "sync_communicate_score",
   300→            actor="system",
   301→            detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   302→        )
   303→    if import_scores_result.changes:
   304→        injected = bool(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", ()))
   305→        pruned = list(getattr(import_scores_result, "pruned", ()))
   306→        append_log_entry(
   307→            plan,
   308→            "sync_import_scores",
   309→            actor="system",
   310→            detail={
   311→                "trigger": "review_import",
   312→                "injected": injected,
   313→                "pruned": pruned,
   314→            },
   315→        )
   316→    if create_plan_result.changes:
   317→        append_log_entry(
   318→            plan,
   319→            "sync_create_plan",
   320→            actor="system",
   321→            detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   322→        )
   323→
   324→
   325→def _append_review_import_sync_log(
   326→    plan: dict,
   327→    diff: dict,
   328→    mutations: _PlanImportMutations,
   329→    *,
   330→    covered_ids: tuple[str, ...],
   331→) -> None:
   332→    if not (
   333→        mutations.import_result is not None
   334→        or mutations.workflow_injected_ids
   335→        or covered_ids
   336→    ):
   337→        return
   338→    import_result = mutations.import_result
   339→    stale_sync_result = mutations.stale_sync_result
   340→    append_log_entry(
   341→        plan,
   342→        "review_import_sync",
   343→        actor="system",
   344→        detail={
   345→            "trigger": "review_import",
   346→            "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   347→            "added_to_queue": (
   348→                import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   349→            ),
   350→            "workflow_injected_ids": mutations.workflow_injected_ids,
   351→            "triage_injected": (
   352→                import_result.triage_injected if import_result is not None else False
   353→            ),
   354→            "triage_injected_ids": (
   355→                import_result.triage_injected_ids if import_result is not None else []
   356→            ),
   357→            "triage_deferred": (
   358→                import_result.triage_deferred if import_result is not None else False
   359→            ),
   360→            "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   361→            "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   362→            "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   363→            "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   364→                import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   365→            ),
   366→            "covered_subjective": list(covered_ids),
   367→            "stale_sync_injected": (
   368→                sorted(stale_sync_result.injected)
   369→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   370→            ),
   371→            "stale_sync_pruned": (
   372→                sorted(stale_sync_result.pruned)
   373→                if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   374→            ),
   375→            "auto_cluster_changes": mutations.auto_cluster_changes,
   376→        },
   377→    )
   378→
   379→
   380→def sync_plan_after_import(
   381→    state: dict,
   382→    diff: dict,
   383→    assessment_mode: str,
   384→    *,
   385→    request: PlanImportSyncRequest | None = None,
   386→) -> None:
   387→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
   388→    try:
   389→        state_file = request.state_file if request is not None else None
   390→        config = request.config if request is not None else None
   391→        import_file = request.import_file if request is not None else None
   392→        import_payload = request.import_payload if request is not None else None
   393→
   394→        plan_path = None
   395→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   396→        if state_file is not None:
   397→            plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   398→        if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   399→            return
   400→
   401→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   402→        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   403→            state,
   404→            target_strict=target_strict,
   405→            plan=plan,
   406→        )
   407→        snapshot = score_snapshot(state)
   408→        current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   409→            strict=snapshot.strict,
   410→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   411→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   412→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   413→        )
   414→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   415→        communicate_result = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   416→            plan,
   417→            state,
   418→            policy=policy,
   419→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   420→            current_scores=current_scores,
   421→        )
   422→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   423→            plan,
   424→            state,
   425→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   426→            import_file=import_file,
   427→            import_payload=import_payload,
   428→        )
   429→        create_plan_result = sync_create_plan_needed(
   430→            plan,
   431→            state,
   432→            policy=policy,
   433→        )
   434→
   435→        sync_inputs = _build_import_sync_inputs(diff, import_payload)
   436→        mutations = _PlanImportMutations()
   437→        _record_workflow_change(
   438→            mutations,
   439→            communicate_result,
   440→            workflow_id="workflow::communicate-score",
   441→        )
   442→        _record_workflow_change(
   443→            mutations,
   444→            import_scores_result,
   445→            workflow_id="workflow::import-scores",
   446→            injected=bool(getattr(import_scores_result, "injected", ())),
   447→        )
   448→        _record_workflow_change(
   449→            mutations,
   450→            create_plan_result,
   451→            workflow_id="workflow::create-plan",
   452→        )
   453→        _sync_review_delta(
   454→            mutations,
   455→            plan,
   456→            state,
   457→            policy=policy,
   458→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   459→        )
   460→        _sync_subjective_queue_after_import(
   461→            mutations,
   462→            plan,
   463→            state,
   464→            policy=policy,
   465→            target_strict=target_strict,
   466→            sync_inputs=sync_inputs,
   467→        )
   468→
   469→        if mutations.dirty:
   470→            if _sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import(plan, state, policy=policy):
   471→                mutations.dirty = True
   472→            _append_workflow_log_entries(
   473→                plan,
   474→                communicate_result=communicate_result,
   475→                import_scores_result=import_scores_result,
   476→                create_plan_result=create_plan_result,
   477→            )
   478→            _append_review_import_sync_log(
   479→                plan,
   480→                diff,
   481→                mutations,
   482→                covered_ids=sync_inputs.covered_ids,
   483→            )
   484→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   485→
   486→        if mutations.import_result is not None:
   487→            _print_review_import_sync(
   488→                state,
   489→                mutations.import_result,
   490→                workflow_injected=bool(mutations.workflow_injected_ids),
   491→            )
   492→        _print_workflow_injected_message(mutations.workflow_injected_ids)
   493→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   494→        print(
   495→            colorize(
   496→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   497→                "dim",
   498→            )
   499→        )
   500→
   501→
   502→__all__ = ["PlanImportSyncRequest", "sync_plan_after_import"]
   503→
```

```
1→"""Helpers for the persisted queue lifecycle phase.
     2→
     3→The persisted phase is the normal source of truth for CLI/debugging, but it is
     4→not trusted blindly. Queue assembly still re-resolves the active phase from the
     5→currently visible items as a safety net so stale saved state cannot strand the
     6→user in the wrong phase after out-of-band changes.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from typing import Iterable
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import counts_toward_objective_backlog
    16→
    17→[REDACTED]
    18→_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY = "lifecycle_phase"
    19→
    20→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    21→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW = "review"
    22→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW = "workflow"
    23→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE = "triage"
    24→LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    25→VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES = frozenset(
    26→    {
    27→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    28→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    29→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    30→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    31→        LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    32→    }
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, object]:
    37→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    38→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    39→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    40→        refresh_state = {}
    41→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    42→    return refresh_state
    43→
    44→
    45→def _is_real_queue_issue(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    46→    return not any(str(issue_id).startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _touches_objective_issue(
    50→    *,
    51→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None,
    52→    state: dict[str, object] | None,
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    if issue_ids is None:
    55→        return True
    56→
    57→    real_issue_ids = [
    58→        issue_id
    59→        for issue_id in issue_ids
    60→        if _is_real_queue_issue(str(issue_id))
    61→    ]
    62→    if not real_issue_ids:
    63→        return False
    64→    if not isinstance(state, dict):
    65→        return True
    66→
    67→    issues = state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})
    68→    if not isinstance(issues, dict):
    69→        return True
    70→
    71→    objective_seen = False
    72→    for issue_id in real_issue_ids:
    73→        issue = issues.get(issue_id)
    74→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    75→            return True
    76→        if counts_toward_objective_backlog(issue):
    77→            objective_seen = True
    78→    return objective_seen
    79→
    80→
    81→def current_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel) -> str | None:
    82→    """Return the persisted lifecycle phase, falling back for legacy plans."""
    83→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    84→    if isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    85→        phase = refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY)
    86→        if isinstance(phase, str) and phase in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    87→            return phase
    88→    if postflight_scan_pending(plan):
    89→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    90→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    91→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    92→    return None
    93→
    94→
    95→def set_lifecycle_phase(plan: PlanModel, phase: str) -> bool:
    96→    """Persist the current queue lifecycle phase."""
    97→    if phase not in VALID_LIFECYCLE_PHASES:
    98→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported lifecycle phase: {phase}")
    99→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   100→    if refresh_state.get(_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY) == phase:
   101→        return False
   102→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = phase
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→def sync_lifecycle_phase(
   107→    plan: PlanModel,
   108→    *,
   109→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   110→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   111→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   112→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   113→    has_triage: bool,
   114→    has_deferred: bool,
   115→) -> tuple[str, bool]:
   116→    """Resolve and persist the current lifecycle phase from queue-state facts."""
   117→    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(
   118→        plan,
   119→        has_initial_reviews=has_initial_reviews,
   120→        has_objective_backlog=has_objective_backlog,
   121→        has_postflight_review=has_postflight_review,
   122→        has_postflight_workflow=has_postflight_workflow,
   123→        has_triage=has_triage,
   124→        has_deferred=has_deferred,
   125→    )
   126→    return phase, set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)
   127→
   128→
   129→def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    *,
   132→    has_initial_reviews: bool,
   133→    has_objective_backlog: bool,
   134→    has_postflight_review: bool,
   135→    has_postflight_workflow: bool,
   136→    has_triage: bool,
   137→    has_deferred: bool,
   138→) -> str:
   139→    """Resolve the lifecycle phase from explicit queue-state facts."""
   140→    if has_initial_reviews:
   141→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   142→    if has_objective_backlog:
   143→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   144→    if has_deferred or postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   145→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   146→    if has_postflight_review:
   147→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
   148→    if has_postflight_workflow:
   149→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
   150→    if has_triage:
   151→        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
   152→    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan)
   153→    if persisted is not None:
   154→        return persisted
   155→    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
   156→
   157→
   158→def postflight_scan_pending(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
   159→    """Return True when the current empty-queue boundary still needs a scan."""
   160→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
   161→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
   162→        return True
   163→    return not isinstance(refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY), int)
   164→
   165→
   166→def mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   167→    plan: PlanModel,
   168→    *,
   169→    scan_count: int | None,
   170→) -> bool:
   171→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current refresh cycle."""
   172→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   173→    try:
   174→        normalized_scan_count = int(scan_count or 0)
   175→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   176→        normalized_scan_count = 0
   177→    if refresh_state.get(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY) == normalized_scan_count:
   178→        return False
   179→    refresh_state[_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY] = normalized_scan_count
   180→    return True
   181→
   182→
   183→def clear_postflight_scan_completion(
   184→    plan: PlanModel,
   185→    *,
   186→    issue_ids: Iterable[str] | None = None,
   187→    state: dict[str, object] | None = None,
   188→) -> bool:
   189→    """Require a fresh scan after queue-changing work on objective issues."""
   190→    if not _touches_objective_issue(issue_ids=issue_ids, state=state):
   191→        return False
   192→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   193→    if _POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY not in refresh_state:
   194→        return False
   195→    refresh_state[_LIFECYCLE_PHASE_KEY] = LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
   196→    refresh_state.pop(_POSTFLIGHT_SCAN_KEY, None)
   197→    return True
   198→
   199→

   201→    "clear_postflight_scan_completion",
   202→    "current_lifecycle_phase",
   203→    "LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE",
```

```
1→"""Plan sync helpers for review-import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    10→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    11→    compute_open_issue_ids,
    12→    sync_triage_needed,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import is_triage_finding
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
    20→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
    21→
    22→    new_ids: set[str]
    23→    added_to_queue: list[str]
    24→    triage_injected: bool
    25→    stale_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    26→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    27→    triage_deferred: bool = False
    28→
    29→
    30→def _has_triage_baseline(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    31→    """Return True when triage has recorded at least one baseline issue ID."""
    32→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    33→    triaged_ids = meta.get("triaged_ids", [])
    34→    return bool(triaged_ids)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
    38→    plan: PlanModel,
    39→    state: StateModel,
    40→    *,
    41→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    42→) -> set[str]:
    43→    """Return review IDs that should be synced into queue_order after import.
    44→
    45→    No triage baseline yet: include all currently-open review IDs so the
    46→    first import cannot drop follow-up work.
    47→    Existing triage baseline: include only IDs that are new since triage.
    48→    """
    49→    if _has_triage_baseline(plan):
    50→        return compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
    51→    return set(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else compute_open_issue_ids(state)
    52→
    53→
    54→def _is_review_queue_id(issue_id: str, state: StateModel) -> bool:
    55→    """Return True for queue IDs representing triage findings.
    56→
    57→    Falls back to legacy ID prefixes only when the issue payload is absent.
    58→    """
    59→    issue = (state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})).get(issue_id)
    60→    if isinstance(issue, dict):
    61→        return is_triage_finding(issue)
    62→    return issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::")
    63→
    64→
    65→def _prune_stale_triage_meta(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    *,
    68→    stale_ids: set[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Drop stale review IDs from triage recovery metadata."""
    71→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta")
    72→    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not stale_ids:
    73→        return
    74→
    75→    active_ids = meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")
    76→    if isinstance(active_ids, list):
    77→        filtered_active = [
    78→            issue_id for issue_id in active_ids
    79→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    80→        ]
    81→        if filtered_active:
    82→            meta["active_triage_issue_ids"] = filtered_active
    83→        else:
    84→            meta.pop("active_triage_issue_ids", None)
    85→
    86→    undispositioned_ids = meta.get("undispositioned_issue_ids")
    87→    if isinstance(undispositioned_ids, list):
    88→        filtered_undispositioned = [
    89→            issue_id for issue_id in undispositioned_ids
    90→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id not in stale_ids
    91→        ]
    92→        if filtered_undispositioned:
    93→            meta["undispositioned_issue_ids"] = filtered_undispositioned
    94→            meta["undispositioned_issue_count"] = len(filtered_undispositioned)
    95→        else:
    96→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_ids", None)
    97→            meta.pop("undispositioned_issue_count", None)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   101→    plan: PlanModel,
   102→    state: StateModel,
   103→    *,
   104→    live_open_review_ids: set[str],
   105→) -> list[str]:
   106→    """Remove stale review IDs from queue-related plan containers.
   107→
   108→    Review imports can auto-resolve prior review IDs. These IDs must be removed
   109→    from queue_order (and linked queue metadata) or the queue can drift from
   110→    state reality.
   111→    """
   112→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   113→    stale_ids = sorted(
   114→        {
   115→            issue_id
   116→            for issue_id in order
   117→            if _is_review_queue_id(issue_id, state) and issue_id not in live_open_review_ids
   118→        }
   119→    )
   120→    if not stale_ids:
   121→        return []
   122→
   123→    stale_set = set(stale_ids)
   124→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in stale_set]
   125→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, stale_set)
   126→    _prune_stale_triage_meta(plan, stale_ids=stale_set)
   127→
   128→    deferred = plan.get("deferred")
   129→    if isinstance(deferred, list):
   130→        plan["deferred"] = [issue_id for issue_id in deferred if issue_id not in stale_set]
   131→
   132→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
   133→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   134→        for issue_id in stale_set:
   135→            skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   136→
   137→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   138→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
   139→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   140→            continue
   141→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
   142→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in stale_set
   143→        ]
   144→
   145→    return stale_ids
   146→
   147→
   148→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→    *,
   152→    policy=None,
   153→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   154→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   155→
   156→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order, prunes stale review IDs from queue
   157→    containers, and injects triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard
   158→    — defers when objective work remains). Returns ``None`` when no queue
   159→    changes are required.
   160→    """
   161→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   162→    open_review_ids = compute_open_issue_ids(state)
   163→    stale_pruned_from_queue = _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   164→        plan,
   165→        state,
   166→        live_open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   167→    )
   168→    new_ids = _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
   169→        plan,
   170→        state,
   171→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   172→    )
   173→    if not new_ids and not stale_pruned_from_queue:
   174→        return None
   175→
   176→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   177→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   178→    existing = set(order)
   179→    added: list[str] = []
   180→    for issue_id in sorted(new_ids):
   181→        if issue_id not in existing:
   182→            order.append(issue_id)
   183→            added.append(issue_id)
   184→
   185→    # Inject triage stages if needed (policy enables mid-cycle guard)
   186→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   187→    triage_injected_ids = list(getattr(triage_result, "injected", []) or [])
   188→    triage_injected = bool(triage_injected_ids)
   189→    triage_deferred = bool(triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "deferred", False))
   190→
   191→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   192→        new_ids=new_ids,
   193→        added_to_queue=added,
   194→        stale_pruned_from_queue=stale_pruned_from_queue,
   195→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   196→        triage_injected_ids=triage_injected_ids,
   197→        triage_deferred=triage_deferred,
   198→    )
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["ReviewImportSyncResult", "sync_plan_after_review_import"]
   202→
```

```
1→"""Canonical queue snapshot for phase and visibility decisions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.cluster_semantics import (
    11→    cluster_allows_ephemeral_execution,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    14→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    15→    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    18→    _tracked_plan_ids as _tracked_plan_ids,
    19→    executable_objective_ids as _executable_objective_ids,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._plan.triage.snapshot import build_triage_snapshot
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.issue_semantics import (
    24→    counts_toward_objective_backlog,
    25→    is_assessment_request,
    26→    is_triage_finding,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    29→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
    30→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    31→    build_subjective_items,
    32→    build_triage_stage_items,
    33→)
    34→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic_workflow import (
    35→    build_communicate_score_item,
    36→    build_create_plan_item,
    37→    build_deferred_disposition_item,
    38→    build_import_scores_item,
    39→    build_run_scan_item,
    40→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    41→)
    42→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    43→
    44→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    45→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    46→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    47→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    48→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    49→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    50→
    51→
    52→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    53→class QueueSnapshot:
    54→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    55→
    56→    phase: str
    57→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    58→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    59→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    60→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    61→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    62→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    63→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    64→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    65→    objective_in_scope_count: int
    66→    planned_objective_count: int
    67→    objective_execution_count: int
    68→    objective_backlog_count: int
    69→    subjective_initial_count: int
    70→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    71→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    72→    triage_pending_count: int
    73→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    74→
    75→
    76→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    77→    if options is None:
    78→        return default
    79→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    83→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    84→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    85→        return state.get("scan_path")
    86→    return scan_path
    87→
    88→
    89→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    90→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    91→        return True
    92→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    93→    if not scores:
    94→        return True
    95→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    96→
    97→
    98→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    99→    return (
   100→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   101→        and counts_toward_objective_backlog(item)
   102→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    return [
   108→        item for item in items
   109→        if is_triage_finding(item)
   110→    ]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _assessment_request_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   114→    return [
   115→        item for item in items
   116→        if is_assessment_request(item)
   117→    ]
   118→
   119→
   120→def _auto_promoted_autofix_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
   121→    """Return auto-cluster member IDs eligible to execute without manual promotion."""
   122→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   123→        return set()
   124→    autofix_ids: set[str] = set()
   125→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   126→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   127→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   128→            continue
   129→        if not cluster_allows_ephemeral_execution(cluster):
   130→            continue
   131→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   132→            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id and issue_id not in skipped_ids:
   133→                autofix_ids.add(issue_id)
   134→    return autofix_ids
   135→
   136→
   137→def _merge_execution_candidates(
   138→    *,
   139→    all_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   140→    explicit_objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   141→    plan: dict | None,
   142→    skipped_ids: set[str],
   143→    review_issue_ids: set[str],
   144→    executable_review_ids: set[str],
   145→    assessment_request_ids: set[str],
   146→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   147→    """Merge plan-anchored and auto-promoted execution candidates."""
   148→    explicit_queue_ids = {
   149→        str(issue_id)
   150→        for issue_id in (plan or {}).get("queue_order", [])
   151→        if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id
   152→    } - skipped_ids
   153→    auto_promoted_ids = _auto_promoted_autofix_ids(plan)
   154→    explicit_queue_ids |= auto_promoted_ids
   155→
   156→    queued_non_review_items = [
   157→        item
   158→        for item in all_issue_items
   159→        if item.get("id", "") in explicit_queue_ids
   160→        and item.get("id", "") not in assessment_request_ids
   161→        and (
   162→            item.get("id", "") not in review_issue_ids
   163→            or item.get("id", "") in executable_review_ids
   164→        )
   165→    ]
   166→
   167→    execution_candidates: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   168→    seen_execution_ids: set[str] = set()
   169→    for item in [*explicit_objective_items, *queued_non_review_items]:
   170→        item_id = str(item.get("id", ""))
   171→        if not item_id or item_id in seen_execution_ids:
   172→            continue
   173→        seen_execution_ids.add(item_id)
   174→        execution_candidates.append(item)
   175→
   176→    anchored_execution_ids = (_tracked_plan_ids(plan) | auto_promoted_ids) - skipped_ids
   177→    anchored_execution_items = [
   178→        item
   179→        for item in execution_candidates
   180→        if item.get("id", "") in anchored_execution_ids
   181→    ]
   182→    return execution_candidates, anchored_execution_items
   183→
   184→
   185→def _executable_review_issue_items(
   186→    plan: dict | None,
   187→    state: StateModel,
   188→    review_issue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   189→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   190→    """Hide raw review findings until triage is current for the live issue set."""
   191→    if not review_issue_items or not isinstance(plan, dict):
   192→        return review_issue_items
   193→
   194→    triage_snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(plan, state)
   195→    if triage_snapshot.has_triage_in_queue:
   196→        return []
   197→    if triage_snapshot.is_triage_stale:
   198→        return []
   199→    if not triage_snapshot.triage_has_run:
   200→        return []
   201→    return review_issue_items
   202→
   203→
   204→def _subjective_partitions(
   205→    state: StateModel,
   206→    *,
   207→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   208→    threshold: float,
   209→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   210→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   211→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   212→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   213→    return initial, postflight
   214→
   215→
   216→def _workflow_partitions(
   217→    plan: dict | None,
   218→    state: StateModel,
   219→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   220→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   221→        return [], [], []
   222→    scan_items = [
   223→        item
   224→        for item in (
   225→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   226→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   227→        )
   228→        if item is not None
   229→    ]
   230→    postflight_workflow = [
   231→        item
   232→        for item in (
   233→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   234→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   235→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   236→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   237→        )
   238→        if item is not None
   239→    ]
   240→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   241→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   242→
   243→
   244→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   245→    *,
   246→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   247→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   248→    anchored_execution_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   249→    explicit_queue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   250→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   251→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   252→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   253→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   254→) -> str:
   255→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   256→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   257→    if anchored_execution_items:
   258→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   259→    if scan_items:
   260→        return PHASE_SCAN
   261→    if explicit_queue_items:
   262→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   263→    if postflight_review_items:
   264→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   265→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   266→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   267→    if triage_items:
   268→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   269→    return PHASE_SCAN
   270→
   271→
   272→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   273→    phase: str,
   274→    *,
   275→    explicit_queue_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   276→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   277→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   278→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   279→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   280→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   281→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   282→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   283→        return initial_review_items
   284→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   285→        return explicit_queue_items
   286→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   287→        deferred_items = [
   288→            item for item in scan_items
   289→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   290→        ]
   291→        if deferred_items:
   292→            return deferred_items
   293→        return [
   294→            item for item in scan_items
   295→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   296→        ]
   297→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   298→        return postflight_review_items
   299→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   300→        return postflight_workflow_items
   301→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   302→        return triage_items
   303→    return []
   304→
   305→
   306→def build_queue_snapshot(
   307→    state: StateModel,
   308→    *,
   309→    options: object | None = None,
   310→    plan: dict | None = None,
   311→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   312→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   313→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   314→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   315→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   316→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   317→    )
   318→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   319→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   320→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues((state.get("work_items") or state.get("issues", {})), scan_path)
   321→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   322→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   323→
   324→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   325→        state,
   326→        scan_path=scan_path,
   327→        status_filter="open",
   328→        scope=scope,
   329→        chronic=chronic,
   330→    )
   331→    objective_items = [
   332→        item for item in all_issue_items
   333→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   334→    ]
   335→    executable_objective_ids = _executable_objective_ids(
   336→        {item.get("id", "") for item in objective_items},
   337→        effective_plan,
   338→    )
   339→    explicit_objective_items = [
   340→        item for item in objective_items
   341→        if item.get("id", "") in executable_objective_ids
   342→    ]
   343→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   344→    assessment_request_items = _assessment_request_items(all_issue_items)
   345→    executable_review_items = _executable_review_issue_items(
   346→        effective_plan,
   347→        state,
   348→        review_issue_items,
   349→    )
   350→    review_issue_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in review_issue_items}
   351→    executable_review_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in executable_review_items}
   352→    assessment_request_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in assessment_request_items}
   353→    explicit_queue_items, anchored_execution_items = _merge_execution_candidates(
   354→        all_issue_items=all_issue_items,
   355→        explicit_objective_items=explicit_objective_items,
   356→        plan=effective_plan,
   357→        skipped_ids=skipped_ids,
   358→        review_issue_ids=review_issue_ids,
   359→        executable_review_ids=executable_review_ids,
   360→        assessment_request_ids=assessment_request_ids,
   361→    )
   362→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   363→        state,
   364→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   365→        threshold=target_strict,
   366→    )
   367→    postflight_review_items = [
   368→        *subjective_postflight_items,
   369→        *assessment_request_items,
   370→        *executable_review_items,
   371→    ]
   372→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   373→        effective_plan,
   374→        state,
   375→    )
   376→
   377→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   378→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   379→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   380→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   381→        anchored_execution_items=anchored_execution_items,
   382→        explicit_queue_items=explicit_queue_items,
   383→        scan_items=scan_items,
   384→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   385→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   386→        triage_items=triage_items,
   387→    )
   388→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   389→        phase,
   390→        explicit_queue_items=explicit_queue_items,
   391→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   392→        scan_items=scan_items,
   393→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   394→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   395→        triage_items=triage_items,
   396→    )
   397→
   398→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   399→    backlog_items = [
   400→        item for item in (
   401→            [
   402→                *objective_items,
   403→                *initial_review_items,
   404→                *subjective_postflight_items,
   405→                *assessment_request_items,
   406→                *review_issue_items,
   407→                *scan_items,
   408→                *postflight_workflow_items,
   409→                *triage_items,
   410→            ]
   411→        )
   412→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   413→    ]
   414→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   415→        1 for item in objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   416→    )
   417→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = len(explicit_objective_items) < len(objective_items)
   418→
   419→    return QueueSnapshot(
   420→        phase=phase,
   421→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   422→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   423→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   424→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   425→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   426→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   427→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   428→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   429→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   430→        planned_objective_count=len(explicit_objective_items),
   431→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   432→            1
   433→            for item in execution_items
   434→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   435→            and counts_toward_objective_backlog(item)
   436→        ),
   437→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   438→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   439→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   440→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   441→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   442→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   443→    )
   444→
   445→
   446→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   447→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   448→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   449→        return "review"
   450→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   451→        return "workflow"
   452→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   453→        return "triage"
   454→    return phase
   455→
   456→
   457→__all__ = [
   458→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   459→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   460→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   461→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   462→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   463→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   464→    "QueueSnapshot",
   465→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   466→    "coarse_phase_name",
   467→]
   468→
```

> AGENT

Now I have a clear picture. Here's my assessment on the three questions:

---

## Will it break anything?

**Yes, one thing the plan doesn't handle:** The `_print_review_import_footer` function at `plan_sync.py:162` currently checks `result.triage_injected` to decide whether to show triage CLI commands:

```python
if result.triage_injected and not workflow_injected:
    print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
    print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
```

With `inject_triage=False`, `mutations.import_result.triage_injected` is **always False**. But the pipeline may have injected triage in its step 6. So users who import reviews that trigger triage will no longer see the triage CLI hints. The footer needs to accept a `triage_injected` flag sourced from the pipeline result, not from `ReviewImportSyncResult`.

**Same problem for the structured log:** `_append_review_import_sync_log` at line 325 reads `triage_injected`, `triage_injected_ids`, and `triage_deferred` from `mutations.import_result`. All three will always be False/empty now. The plan says "triage remains tracked separately by pipeline result" but never says where/how these fields get into the log entry. The log function needs to accept pipeline triage fields alongside import results.

## Anything unintentional?

**One ordering change is unacknowledged.** Currently in `sync_plan_after_import`, workflow gates (communicate-score, import-scores, create-plan) run **before** `_sync_review_delta`. The plan reverses this — review delta runs first (step 3), then the pipeline runs workflow gates (step 6). I checked the gate predicates: none depend on `queue_order` contents that review delta mutates (they check policy dimensions, plan_start_scores, assessment_mode), so this is **safe** — but it should be called out as an intentional change rather than leaving it as an implicit side effect.

**The lifecycle unification is slightly richer than what's documented.** Looking at the actual resolution functions:

The import path's `resolve_lifecycle_phase` checks `has_deferred` (temporary skips) for SCAN. The snapshot's `_phase_for_snapshot` checks `scan_items` which comes from `build_deferred_disposition_item(plan)`. These should be equivalent, but they're different code paths checking the same intent — worth a test that pins the equivalence.

The one **actually desirable** behavioral change: mid-cycle with unscored dimensions. The old import path returns REVIEW unconditionally (`has_initial_reviews=True`). The snapshot requires `fresh_boundary AND initial_review_items`, so mid-cycle it stays in EXECUTE. This is correct — you shouldn't regress to REVIEW mid-cycle — but it changes behavior for users.

## Will it be elegant?

**The pipeline itself: yes.** Single function, explicit skip flags, clean dataclass results, correct separation of concerns. It's a nice design.

**The callers: mixed.** The scan caller gets cleaner — the `_display_pipeline_results` / `_log_pipeline_changes` pair is a better pattern than six individual `_sync_*_and_log` wrappers. But the import caller stays complex. It still needs `_PlanImportMutations`, `_merge_pipeline_workflow_results`, `_record_workflow_change`, `_build_import_sync_inputs`, `_sync_review_delta`, plus now the triage-field plumbing I mentioned above. The total line count won't drop much — it's more of a correctness and single-ownership win than a simplification win.

**One thing that would make it more elegant:** instead of `_merge_pipeline_workflow_results` pushing pipeline results into `_PlanImportMutations` and then `_append_review_import_sync_log` reading them back out, the log function could just accept the `PostflightResult` directly. The mutations struct would only carry import-delta-specific state, and the log function would merge both sources. Cleaner data flow, fewer intermediate copies.

---

```
   279→
   280→## Assumptions And Defaults
   281→
   282→- Lifecycle unification on snapshot phase is intentional and accepted as a behavior change.
   283→- `sync_plan_after_review_import(..., inject_triage=False)` is only for orchestrator use; engine direct callers keep default behavior.
   284→- The pipeline is postflight-only. Scan-specific plan-start-score and postflight-scan completion logic remain outside it.
   285→- No gate predicate or queue mutation semantics change beyond triage ownership and lifecycle unification (see Known Behavioral Deltas for the full list of expected changes).
   286→- Incremental implementation order:
   287→  1. make review-import triage optional
   288→  2. add pipeline module and exports
   289→  3. refactor scan caller
   290→  4. refactor import caller
   291→  5. update/add tests
   292→
```

The new flow reverses this: review delta runs first (step 3), then the pipeline runs workflow gates (step 6). This is safe — none of the workflow gate predicates depend on `queue_order` contents that review delta mutates (they check policy dimensions, `plan_start_scores`, `assessment_mode`) — but it is an intentional change, not an accidental side effect.\n\n### Auto-clustering gate asymmetry"
```json
}
```

Done. Here's what I changed:

1. **Triage display/logging fix** — `_print_review_import_footer` and `_append_review_import_sync_log` now source triage fields from `PostflightResult`, not from `ReviewImportSyncResult` (which is always False/empty with `inject_triage=False`). The "keep" list now spells out each `_print_*` function and marks the two that need updates.

2. **Workflow gate ordering** — new behavioral delta section documenting that workflow gates now run after review delta (safe, but intentional).

3. **Mid-cycle unscored dimensions** — new behavioral delta documenting the REVIEW→EXECUTE change.

4. **"Real queue drained" definition** — tightened from vague to explicit: means objective execution queue empty, not "any tracked work exists."

5. **Phase invariants** — new section near the pipeline order: review findings stay postflight, assessment requests stay postflight, only objective mutations reopen scan boundary, all surfaces use canonical snapshot.

6. **Test cases** — added phase isolation test (objective + review findings = only objective in execution) and scan boundary test (postflight review mutation doesn't bounce to run-scan).

7. **Assumptions** — updated to acknowledge scan-boundary reopening is part of the contract.

8. **Import flow steps** — renumbered and clarified: step 7 is `_merge_pipeline_into_mutations`, step 9 passes `PostflightResult` to the log function, step 10 passes `triage_injected` from pipeline to the footer.

> AGENT

OK. Here's what's wrong with the plan as written, and what would make it genuinely simpler.

---

**Problem 1: The `_PlanImportMutations` adapter layer is unnecessary complexity.**

The plan keeps `_PlanImportMutations`, adds `_merge_pipeline_into_mutations` to copy pipeline results into it, then has `_append_review_import_sync_log` read from *both* mutations and the pipeline result. This is a translation layer between two data shapes that serves no purpose — the pipeline result already has everything.

The fix: kill `_PlanImportMutations`, `_merge_pipeline_into_mutations`, and `_record_workflow_change`. The import flow just holds two values: `import_result: ReviewImportSyncResult | None` (from review delta) and `pipeline_result: PostflightResult` (from pipeline). Pass both directly to display and logging functions. No intermediate struct, no copying fields between dataclasses.

**Problem 2: The pipeline produces data but the callers re-implement logging.**

The scan caller needs `_log_pipeline_changes` to write 6 `append_log_entry` calls. The import caller needs updated `_append_review_import_sync_log` plus deleted `_append_workflow_log_entries`. But `append_log_entry` is in `engine._plan.operations.meta` — it's engine-layer. The pipeline can call it directly without violating "no app imports."

The fix: the pipeline writes its own per-gate log entries (same action names, same detail shapes as today). Callers only write caller-specific logs: scan writes `seed_start_scores`/`clear_start_scores`/`complete_postflight_scan`, import writes `review_import_sync`. This eliminates `_log_pipeline_changes` entirely and simplifies the import logging.

**Problem 3: Display wrappers are specified but not simplified.**

`_display_pipeline_results` for scan is essentially the same 6 print blocks from the deleted `_sync_*_and_log` functions, just reading from a result struct instead of return values. It's the same amount of code, rearranged.

This one is harder to simplify — scan and import genuinely have different display needs. But the scan display wrapper can be just one function with a sequence of `if result.subjective and result.subjective.changes: print(...)` blocks. That's straightforward enough. The concern is more about the plan's phrasing implying more structure than needed.

```
1→# Centralize Postflight Pipeline
     2→
     3→## Summary
     4→
     5→Create a single engine-level postflight pipeline for the shared "objective execution queue drained -> inject follow-up work" sequence, and have both scan reconciliation and review-import sync call it.
     6→
     7→"Real queue drained" means the **objective execution queue** is empty — not "any tracked work exists" and not "triage has completed for review findings." Postflight work (review, workflow, triage) is follow-up that triggers only after objective items are resolved.
     8→
     9→This design works with these constraints:
    10→
    11→- The pipeline owns triage, otherwise the import path can still lose `workflow::create-plan`.
    12→- `target_strict` is always passed explicitly from callers.
    13→- `plan_start_scores` and postflight-scan completion stay in the scan command, not in engine pipeline code.
    14→- Lifecycle unification on `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase` is an intentional behavior change and must be tested as such.
    15→
    16→## Known Behavioral Deltas
    17→
    18→This unification intentionally changes some behaviors. These must be tested explicitly.
    19→
    20→### Lifecycle phase resolution
    21→
    22→The current post-import path (`_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import`) uses a hand-rolled resolution with explicit flags: `has_initial_reviews`, `has_objective_backlog`, `has_postflight_review`, `has_postflight_workflow`, `has_triage`, `has_deferred`. The unified pipeline replaces this with `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase`.
    23→
    24→Expected deltas to verify:
    25→
    26→- **Deferred/temporary skips:** The import path currently checks `has_deferred` (temporary skip entries in `plan.skipped`) to resolve SCAN phase. Confirm `queue_context` snapshot replicates this or document the acceptable change.
    27→- **Assessment requests in state:** The import path checks `has_postflight_review` which includes assessment requests in state (not just policy unscored/stale). Confirm snapshot.phase accounts for this or accept the narrowing.
    28→- **Conditional execution:** The import path only syncs lifecycle when mutations are dirty. The pipeline always runs lifecycle (Step 7). Guard inside the pipeline: only persist and set `lifecycle_phase_changed=True` when the phase actually differs from the current persisted value, to avoid spurious log entries.
    29→- **Mid-cycle unscored dimensions:** The old import path returns REVIEW unconditionally when `policy.unscored_ids` is non-empty. The snapshot requires `fresh_boundary AND initial_review_items`, so mid-cycle it stays in EXECUTE. This is the correct behavior — mid-cycle shouldn't regress to REVIEW just because a new dimension appeared.
    30→
    31→### Workflow gate ordering
    32→
    33→The current `sync_plan_after_import` runs workflow gates (communicate-score, import-scores, create-plan) **before** `_sync_review_delta`. The new flow reverses this: review delta runs first (step 3), then the pipeline runs workflow gates (step 6). This is safe — none of the workflow gate predicates depend on `queue_order` contents that review delta mutates (they check policy dimensions, `plan_start_scores`, `assessment_mode`) — but it is an intentional change, not an accidental side effect.
    34→
    35→### Auto-clustering gate asymmetry
    36→
    37→The current post-import path **always** runs auto-clustering when there's a data change (no mid-cycle guard). The pipeline uses `skip_auto_cluster`, and the import caller passes `skip_auto_cluster=not has_data_change`. This preserves the "only cluster when data changed" behavior but is worth noting as a formalization — previously it was implicit via the conditional call site.
    38→
    39→## Implementation Changes
    40→
    41→### 1. Make review-import delta triage-optional
    42→
    43→Update `desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py` so `sync_plan_after_review_import(...)` takes `inject_triage: bool = True`.
    44→
    45→Behavior:
    46→
    47→- Default stays `True` so direct engine tests and existing callers keep current semantics.
    48→- When `inject_triage=False`, the function still:
    49→  - prunes stale review IDs from plan containers
    50→  - appends new review IDs to `queue_order`
    51→  - returns `ReviewImportSyncResult`
    52→- But it does not call `sync_triage_needed(...)`, and returns:
    53→  - `triage_injected=False`
    54→  - `triage_injected_ids=[]`
    55→  - `triage_deferred=False`
    56→
    57→This is the prerequisite that lets the new pipeline preserve `create-plan before triage`.
    58→
    59→### 2. Add engine postflight pipeline
    60→
    61→Create `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py` with two dataclasses and one public function.
    62→
    63→Public types:
    64→
    65→- `PostflightContext`
    66→  - `scores_just_imported: bool = False`
    67→  - `current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None`
    68→  - `assessment_mode: str | None = None`
    69→  - `import_file: str | None = None`
    70→  - `import_payload: dict | None = None`
    71→- `PostflightResult`
    72→  - `subjective: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
    73→  - `auto_cluster_changes: int = 0`
    74→  - `communicate_score: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
    75→  - `import_scores: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
    76→  - `create_plan: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
    77→  - `triage: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
    78→  - `lifecycle_phase: str = ""`
    79→  - `lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False`
    80→  - `dirty` property aggregates all changes
    81→  - `workflow_injected_ids` property: collects IDs from `communicate_score`, `import_scores`, and `create_plan` results that were injected, for structured logging by callers
    82→
    83→Public function:
    84→
    85→- `run_postflight_gates(plan, state, *, policy, target_strict, cycle_just_completed=False, context=None, skip_subjective=False, skip_auto_cluster=False) -> PostflightResult`
    86→
    87→Pipeline order:
    88→
    89→1. `sync_subjective_dimensions(...)` unless `skip_subjective`
    90→2. `auto_cluster_issues(...)` unless `skip_auto_cluster`
    91→3. `sync_communicate_score_needed(...)`
    92→4. `sync_import_scores_needed(...)` only if `context.assessment_mode` is set
    93→5. `sync_create_plan_needed(...)`
    94→6. `sync_triage_needed(...)`
    95→7. Resolve lifecycle via `coarse_phase_name(queue_context(state, plan=plan, target_strict=target_strict).snapshot.phase)` and persist with `set_lifecycle_phase(...)`
    96→
    97→Phase invariants the pipeline must preserve:
    98→
    99→- Triaged review findings remain `postflight_review` — they must not be merged into execute-phase candidates while objective work remains.
   100→- Assessment requests also remain postflight-only.
   101→- Only objective-queue mutations may reopen the postflight scan boundary (clear the scan-complete marker). Postflight review / workflow / triage mutations must not.
   102→- All queue surfaces (`next`, `plan queue`, breakdown/progress) must consume the same canonical execution snapshot.
   103→
   104→Important details:
   105→
   106→- The pipeline stays engine-only. No app-layer imports.
   107→- The pipeline writes its own log entries via `append_log_entry` for each gate that fires. Same action names and detail shapes as the current `_sync_*_and_log` wrappers: `sync_subjective`, `auto_cluster`, `sync_communicate_score`, `sync_import_scores`, `sync_create_plan`, `sync_triage`, `sync_lifecycle_phase`. This eliminates the need for caller-side log wrappers entirely — callers only write caller-specific log entries (scan: `seed_start_scores`/`clear_start_scores`/`complete_postflight_scan`; import: `review_import_sync`).
   108→- `queue_context(...)` should receive `target_strict=target_strict` explicitly so snapshot decisions match caller policy.
   109→- `_has_postflight_review_work()` is deleted rather than moved.
   110→- Step 4 (`sync_import_scores_needed`) is naturally a no-op when `context.assessment_mode` is None — scan callers never set this, so no conditional needed at the call site.
   111→- Step 7 (lifecycle) should only persist and set `lifecycle_phase_changed=True` when the resolved phase differs from the currently persisted value, to avoid spurious log entries when nothing changed.
   112→
   113→Export from `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/__init__.py`:
   114→
   115→- `PostflightContext`
   116→- `PostflightResult`
   117→- `run_postflight_gates`
   118→
   119→### 3. Refactor scan reconciliation to call pipeline
   120→
   121→In `desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`:
   122→
   123→Delete the scan-local gate wrappers that are replaced by pipeline output handling:
```
