```
1→# Unify Plan Reconciliation
     2→
     3→## Summary
     4→
     5→The plan queue has exactly two boundary transitions:
     6→
     7→1. **Queue starts** — fresh boundary, no active cycle → inject initial subjective reviews
     8→2. **Queue clears** — canonical executable objective work is empty → inject postflight work (communicate-score, create-plan, triage, stale dimension reruns)
     9→
    10→Reconciliation runs at these two boundaries and nowhere else. Everything between them is just executing work items — no gates fire, no workflow items inject, no queue composition changes.
    11→
    12→A single engine-level function handles both boundaries:
    13→
    14→```
    15→reconcile_plan(plan, state, *, target_strict)
    16→```
    17→
    18→Three arguments. No flags. No context object. It detects which boundary (if any) was reached and acts accordingly. If neither boundary was reached, it's a no-op.
    19→
    20→Scan and import are just the events that can *cause* a boundary transition. After data ingestion (adding findings, syncing review deltas), the caller checks whether a boundary was crossed and calls `reconcile_plan` only if so.
    21→
    22→```
    23→data ingestion  →  boundary crossed?  →  reconcile  →  post-step  →  save  →  display
    24→(caller-specific)     (check)            (shared)     (caller-specific)
    25→```
    26→
    27→## Prerequisite: Canonical Executable Objective Work
    28→
    29→Before this reconciliation can be correct, the system needs a single canonical definition of what constitutes executable objective work. This is the foundation — if boundary detection is wrong, the pipeline fires at the wrong time.
    30→
    31→### The problem
    32→
    33→"Executable objective work" is currently split across:
    34→
    35→- **`queue_order`** in `schema/__init__.py` — the ordered list of issue IDs
    36→- **`overrides`** and **`clusters`** in the plan — manual promotions and grouped work that track real objective items
    37→- **Phase resolution** in `snapshot.py` — `_phase_for_snapshot` which determines whether we're in EXECUTE
    38→
    39→These can drift out of sync. The reigh bug is the canonical example:
    40→
    41→- The plan still owned real objective work through overrides/manual clusters
    42→- `queue_order` no longer listed those items
    43→- Phase logic interpreted that as "objective work drained"
    44→- `next` fell into postflight even though actionable work still existed
    45→
    46→### The invariant
    47→
    48→**Postflight gates may run only when canonical executable objective work is empty.** Canonical executable objective work must recover plan-owned work from overrides and manual cluster state, not just `queue_order`. If the plan tracks an objective item via any ownership mechanism (queue_order, overrides, manual clusters), that item is executable objective work.
    49→
    50→### The fix
    51→
    52→`_phase_for_snapshot` already computes `anchored_execution_items` from `_tracked_plan_ids(plan) | auto_promoted_ids`. This is the canonical set. The boundary check for reconciliation must use this same set — not `queue_order` shape alone, not a separate helper with different logic.
    53→
    54→### Failure mode to guard against
    55→
    56→`queue_order` can drift from tracked plan ownership (items removed from queue_order but still in overrides/clusters). Lifecycle must not treat that drift as objective completion. The boundary check and all queue surfaces must agree on one definition.
    57→
    58→## Design Principles
    59→
    60→- **Two entry points, not two callers.** The entry points are "queue starts" and "queue clears" — not "after scan" and "after import." Scan and import are just events that can trigger a boundary check.
    61→- **The pipeline doesn't know what triggered it.** It derives which boundary was reached from plan + state. Trigger-specific context is expressed as direct plan mutations before the boundary check, not as parameters.
    62→- **Boundary detection uses canonical execution.** "Queue clears" means `anchored_execution_items` is empty — the same set that `_phase_for_snapshot` uses. Not `queue_order` length, not a separate predicate.
    63→- **Gates are self-gating.** Each gate checks its own preconditions. At queue-start, postflight gates don't fire (reviews incomplete). At queue-clear, initial-review gates don't fire (already reviewed). Mid-cycle, nothing fires.
    64→- **Items only show in their phase.** Phase determines execution visibility. Workflow items injected at queue-clear are invisible during EXECUTE. Review findings are invisible during EXECUTE. The snapshot enforces this — `_execution_items_for_phase` returns only items for the current phase. Items cannot leak across phase boundaries.
    65→- **Predetermined order within each phase.** Workflow items run in fixed order: communicate-score → import-scores → create-plan. Then triage stages. `normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix` enforces this in `queue_order`.
    66→- **Triggers are one-time by construction.** Pre-step mutations are idempotent plan state changes (clearing sentinels, writing pending metadata). A second reconcile call is a no-op.
    67→- **The pipeline owns its own logging.** `append_log_entry` is engine-layer. Per-gate log entries are written inside the pipeline. Callers only write caller-specific logs.
    68→
    69→## Known Behavioral Deltas
    70→
    71→### Lifecycle phase resolution
    72→
    73→The current post-import path (`_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import`) uses hand-rolled flags: `has_initial_reviews`, `has_objective_backlog`, `has_postflight_review`, `has_postflight_workflow`, `has_triage`, `has_deferred`. The pipeline replaces this with `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase`.
    74→
    75→Expected deltas to verify:
    76→
    77→- **Deferred/temporary skips:** Import path checks `has_deferred` → SCAN. Confirm snapshot replicates via `build_deferred_disposition_item`.
    78→- **Assessment requests:** Import path checks `has_postflight_review` including assessment requests in state. Confirm snapshot captures these in `postflight_review_items`.
    79→- **Mid-cycle unscored dimensions:** Import path returns REVIEW when `policy.unscored_ids` is non-empty. Snapshot requires `fresh_boundary AND initial_review_items`, so mid-cycle stays in EXECUTE. This is correct — mid-cycle shouldn't regress to REVIEW.
    80→- **Lifecycle only persists on change.** Import path only syncs lifecycle when mutations are dirty. Pipeline always runs lifecycle but only persists when the phase actually differs from the current value.
    81→
    82→### Workflow gate ordering
    83→
    84→Current import path runs workflow gates before review delta. New flow: review delta runs in the pre-step, then the pipeline runs workflow gates. Safe — no workflow gate predicates depend on `queue_order` contents that review delta mutates.
    85→
    86→### Communicate-score bypass narrowing
    87→
    88→Current import path passes `scores_just_imported=True` to bypass the `_no_unscored` check. New design: import pre-step clears `previous_plan_start_scores` sentinel, and the gate fires on its own preconditions. If a trusted import scores all dimensions (the normal case), `_no_unscored` returns True naturally. If a partial trusted import leaves dimensions unscored, communicate-score waits for remaining reviews — a behavioral narrowing. Pin with a test.
    89→
    90→## Implementation Changes
    91→
    92→### 1. Make review-import delta triage-optional
    93→
    94→Update `sync_plan_after_review_import(...)` in `desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py` to take `inject_triage: bool = True`.
    95→
    96→- Default `True` preserves behavior for direct engine callers and tests.
    97→- When `False`: still prunes stale IDs, appends new IDs, returns `ReviewImportSyncResult` — but does not call `sync_triage_needed(...)`. Returns `triage_injected=False`, `triage_injected_ids=[]`, `triage_deferred=False`.
    98→
    99→This is the prerequisite that lets the pipeline run `create-plan` before `triage`.
   100→
   101→### 2. Add internal mid-cycle guard to auto-clustering
   102→
   103→Update `auto_cluster_issues(...)` in `desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py` to return 0 immediately when `is_mid_cycle(plan)` is True.
   104→
   105→Currently the mid-cycle guard lives in the scan caller (`skip_auto_cluster=mid_cycle`). Moving it into the function makes auto-clustering self-gating, which eliminates the last skip flag from the pipeline.
   106→
   107→### 3. Add the reconciliation pipeline
   108→
   109→Create `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`.
   110→
   111→Public type:
   112→
   113→- `ReconcileResult`
   114→  - `subjective: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
   115→  - `auto_cluster_changes: int = 0`
   116→  - `communicate_score: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
   117→  - `create_plan: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
   118→  - `triage: QueueSyncResult | None = None`
   119→  - `lifecycle_phase: str = ""`
   120→  - `lifecycle_phase_changed: bool = False`
   121→  - `dirty` property: True if any gate changed anything
   122→  - `workflow_injected_ids` property: IDs from `communicate_score` and `create_plan` that were injected
   123→
   124→Public function:
   125→
   126→```python
   127→def reconcile_plan(plan, state, *, target_strict) -> ReconcileResult
   128→```
   129→
   130→Internal pipeline order:
   131→
   132→1. Compute `policy = compute_subjective_visibility(state, target_strict, plan)`
   133→2. Compute `cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`
   134→3. Compute `current_scores` from `score_snapshot(state)`
   135→4. `sync_subjective_dimensions(plan, state, policy=policy, cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed)`
   136→5. `auto_cluster_issues(plan, state, target_strict=target_strict, policy=policy)` — self-gating, no-op mid-cycle
   137→6. `sync_communicate_score_needed(plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores)` — no `scores_just_imported` param; sentinel clearing is the pre-step's job
   138→7. `sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)`
   139→8. `sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)`
   140→9. Resolve lifecycle: `coarse_phase_name(queue_context(state, plan=plan, target_strict=target_strict).snapshot.phase)` → `set_lifecycle_phase(plan, phase)`, only persist if changed
   141→
   142→After each gate that fires, write a log entry via `append_log_entry`. Same action names and detail shapes as the current `_sync_*_and_log` wrappers.
   143→
   144→Phase invariants the pipeline must preserve:
   145→
   146→- Triaged review findings remain `postflight_review` — not merged into execute-phase while objective work remains.
   147→- Assessment requests remain postflight-only.
   148→- Only objective-queue mutations may reopen the postflight scan boundary.
   149→- All queue surfaces (`next`, `plan queue`, breakdown/progress) consume the same canonical execution snapshot.
   150→

   152→
   153→- `ReconcileResult`
   154→- `reconcile_plan`

   190→- Flat sequence of `if result.X and result.X.changes: print(...)` blocks.

   192→

Let me chronologically analyze the conversation:

1. **Initial request**: User asked to sense-check a plan document at `tasks/centralize-postflight-pipeline.md` for unifying post-flight and pre-flight processes. They wanted to know if it captures everything, has holes, or misses anything.

6. **"Is this beautiful?"** - User pushed back on elegance. I identified three problems:
   - `_PlanImportMutations` adapter layer unnecessary
   - Pipeline produces data but callers re-implement logging
   - Display wrappers not simplified
   
   Applied simplifications:
   - Pipeline owns logging (append_log_entry is engine-layer)
   - Killed `_PlanImportMutations` and adapter layer

7. **"Is this a beautiful solution?"** - User said no. I identified deeper issues:

8. **"That's not beautiful"** - User rejected both options. I arrived at the key insight: **the pipeline shouldn't need to know what triggered it.** Everything derivable from plan + state. Proposed `reconcile_plan(plan, state, *, target_strict)` — three arguments.

9. **"Does it not need to know if it's pre or post flight"** - User questioned phase awareness. I explained gates are self-gating — phase is an output, not an input. Gates check their own preconditions from plan/state.

10. **"Will you be able to do this without losing functionality? Will triggers be clean and one-time?"** - I traced trigger-specific parameters:

    - `sync_import_scores_needed` → import-only, moves to pre-step
    - `auto_cluster_issues` → needs internal mid-cycle guard
    
    Result: truly three arguments, no flags.

11. **"Let's do it"** - Full rewrite of the doc around new architecture.

12. **"So it'll just be triggered at specific points?"** - I explained it runs after every mutation. User corrected me forcefully.

13. **"WHEN WE START AND WHEN THE QUEUE HAS BEEN CLEARED"** - User clarified: two boundary transitions only. I updated the doc.

14. **"Items in that phase, only they show? Run in predetermined order? Can't show outside this phase?"** - I confirmed all three invariants.

15. **"So you have clear entry points that can only be triggered at those points?"** - I initially said "after scan" and "after import" — user corrected me again.

16. **"HOW CLEAR CAN I GET?"** - User was frustrated. The entry points are queue-start and queue-clear, not scan/import.

17. **Architectural expansion from user's feedback**: User (via brother's suggestion) identified that the doc was too narrow — the real issue is split ownership of "what is live execution work" across schema, snapshot, and mutation paths. Added:

- `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py` — workflow gates
- `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py` — queue snapshot/phase resolution

The final architecture:
- `reconcile_plan(plan, state, *, target_strict)` — three arguments, no flags
- Two boundary transitions: queue starts, queue clears
- Canonical execution set defines boundaries
- Gates are self-gating

- Pipeline owns logging

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked to sense-check and iteratively refine a plan document (`tasks/centralize-postflight-pipeline.md`) for unifying the post-flight and pre-flight plan reconciliation processes in the desloppify codebase. Through extensive back-and-forth, the user pushed for increasingly elegant architecture, ultimately arriving at a design centered on two queue boundary transitions (queue starts, queue clears) with a single three-argument reconciliation function. The user's final questions were about whether the doc clearly shows what it replaces and whether any functionality would be lost.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Queue boundary transitions**: Two entry points — queue starts (fresh boundary) and queue clears (objective work drained)
   - **Canonical executable objective work**: Defined by `_tracked_plan_ids(plan) | auto_promoted_ids`, not `queue_order` alone. Prevents the "reigh bug" where queue_order drifts from plan ownership
   - **Self-gating gates**: Each gate checks its own preconditions from plan + state. No caller-side skip flags needed
   - **Phase isolation**: Items only show in their phase. Workflow items injected at queue-clear are invisible during EXECUTE. Enforced by `_execution_items_for_phase`
   - **Predetermined phase order**: communicate-score → import-scores → create-plan → triage stages. Enforced by `normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix`
   - **Trigger-specific context as plan mutations**: Instead of passing `scores_just_imported=True`, the import pre-step clears `previous_plan_start_scores` sentinel directly
   - **Pipeline owns logging**: `append_log_entry` is engine-layer, so per-gate logs are written inside the pipeline, not by callers
   - **Boundary detection**: Uses the same canonical execution set as `_phase_for_snapshot` — `anchored_execution_items` empty means at boundary

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`tasks/centralize-postflight-pipeline.md`** (fully rewritten multiple times)
     - This is the central plan document. It was rewritten from a "postflight pipeline" framing to a "plan reconciliation at queue boundaries" framing
     - Final version has sections: Summary, Prerequisite (canonical execution), Design Principles, Known Behavioral Deltas, Implementation Changes (6 steps), Test Plan, Assumptions, Implementation Order
     - Core function signature: `reconcile_plan(plan, state, *, target_strict) -> ReconcileResult`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`** (read, to be refactored)
     - Contains the scan-side reconciliation: `reconcile_plan_post_scan()`, `_sync_post_scan_with_policy()`, and 6 `_sync_*_and_log` wrapper functions
     - Key function `_sync_post_scan_with_policy` runs gates sequentially: subjective → auto-cluster (mid-cycle gated) → communicate-score → create-plan → triage → plan-start scores → postflight completion → lifecycle
     - `_is_mid_cycle_scan` checks `is_mid_cycle(plan)` AND queue has non-skipped items
     - Functions to DELETE: `_sync_subjective_and_log`, `_sync_auto_clusters_and_log`, `_sync_communicate_score_and_log`, `_sync_create_plan_and_log`, `_sync_triage_and_log`, `_sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log`, `_has_postflight_review_work`, `_has_postflight_workflow_items`, `_has_triage_items`
     - Functions to KEEP: `_reset_cycle_for_force_rescan`, `_sync_post_scan_without_policy`, `_is_mid_cycle_scan`, `_seed_plan_start_scores`, `_clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty`, `_has_objective_cycle`, `_mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready`, `_sync_plan_start_scores_and_log`, `_sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log`

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`** (read, to be refactored)
     - Contains import-side sync: `sync_plan_after_import()`, `_sync_review_delta()`, `_sync_subjective_queue_after_import()`, etc.
     - Currently runs workflow gates BEFORE review delta — the new design reverses this (safe, documented as behavioral delta)
     - Functions to DELETE: `_has_postflight_review_work`, `_has_deferred_disposition_work`, `_sync_lifecycle_phase_after_import`, `_sync_subjective_queue_after_import`, `_append_workflow_log_entries`, `_PlanImportMutations`, `_record_workflow_change`
     - Functions to KEEP (with updates): `_sync_review_delta`, `_print_review_import_footer` (needs `triage_injected` from pipeline), `_print_review_import_sync`, `_append_review_import_sync_log` (reads from `import_result` + `pipeline_result` directly)
     - New import flow: setup → data ingestion (review delta, import-scores, clear sentinel) → boundary check → reconcile → save → log → display

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py`** (read, to be modified)
     - Contains `sync_plan_after_review_import()` — needs `inject_triage: bool = True` parameter added
     - When `inject_triage=False`: still prunes stale IDs, appends new IDs, returns `ReviewImportSyncResult`, but does NOT call `sync_triage_needed()`. Returns `triage_injected=False`, `triage_injected_ids=[]`, `triage_deferred=False`

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/refresh_lifecycle.py`** (read, important for understanding lifecycle)
     - Contains `sync_lifecycle_phase()` with hand-rolled resolution using explicit flags (6 boolean params)
     - Contains `set_lifecycle_phase()` which already returns False if phase unchanged (guards against spurious persistence)
     - Contains `resolve_lifecycle_phase()` priority cascade: initial_reviews → objective_backlog → deferred/scan_pending → postflight_review → postflight_workflow → triage → fallback
     - The pipeline replaces the hand-rolled resolution with `queue_context(...).snapshot.phase`

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py`** (read, important for gate analysis)
     - Contains `sync_communicate_score_needed()` — currently takes `scores_just_imported` and `current_scores` params. The new design: pipeline computes `current_scores` from `score_snapshot(state)`, import pre-step clears `previous_plan_start_scores` instead of passing `scores_just_imported=True`
     - Contains `sync_import_scores_needed()` — import-only gate, stays in import pre-step, NOT in pipeline
     - Contains `sync_create_plan_needed()` — checks `_no_unscored`, `has_objective_backlog`, not already in queue, no triage stages pending
     - Key: `scores_just_imported` bypasses two checks: (1) `previous_plan_start_scores` sentinel, (2) `_no_unscored`. Removing the bypass is a documented behavioral narrowing

   - **`desloppify/engine/_work_queue/snapshot.py`** (read, critical for phase resolution)
     - Contains `build_queue_snapshot()`, `_phase_for_snapshot()`, `coarse_phase_name()`
     - `_phase_for_snapshot` priority: fresh_boundary+initial_reviews → anchored_execution → scan_items → explicit_queue → postflight_review → postflight_workflow → triage → SCAN
     - `anchored_execution_items` from `(_tracked_plan_ids(plan) | auto_promoted_ids) - skipped_ids` — THIS is the canonical execution set
     - `coarse_phase_name` maps internal phases to persisted values (e.g., PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL → "review")

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/pipeline.py`** (to be created)
     - New file containing `ReconcileResult` dataclass and `reconcile_plan()` function
     - Pipeline order: compute policy → compute cycle_just_completed → compute current_scores → sync_subjective_dimensions → auto_cluster_issues → sync_communicate_score_needed → sync_create_plan_needed → sync_triage_needed → resolve lifecycle
     - Writes its own log entries via `append_log_entry`

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **`_print_review_import_footer` triage display bug**: With `inject_triage=False`, `result.triage_injected` from `ReviewImportSyncResult` is always False, so triage CLI hints would never show even when the pipeline injected triage. Fix: add `triage_injected` parameter sourced from pipeline result.
   - **`_append_review_import_sync_log` triage logging gap**: Same issue — triage fields (`triage_injected`, `triage_injected_ids`, `triage_deferred`) from `mutations.import_result` always False/empty. Fix: log function takes `pipeline_result` directly and reads triage fields from it.
   - **User feedback on "after scan/after import" framing**: User strongly corrected me multiple times ("HOW CLEAR CAN I GET?") — the entry points are queue-start and queue-clear, NOT scan and import. Scan/import are just events that can cause boundary transitions.
   - **User feedback on skip flags**: User rejected designs with skip flags (`skip_subjective`, `skip_auto_cluster`) and context objects (`PostflightContext`) as not beautiful.
   - **User feedback on `_PlanImportMutations` adapter**: Pointed out this was unnecessary complexity — eliminated in favor of passing `import_result` and `pipeline_result` directly.
   - **User feedback on broader architectural issue**: The doc was "too narrow" — focused on gate ordering when the real issue is canonical execution ownership. Added Prerequisite section about the reigh bug and queue_order drift.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Triage ordering bug**: The original motivation — import path could lose `workflow::create-plan` because triage was injected inside `sync_plan_after_review_import` before create-plan. Solved by `inject_triage=False` + pipeline running create-plan before triage.
   - **Lifecycle resolution divergence**: Import path used hand-rolled flags, scan path used snapshot.phase. Unified on snapshot.phase with documented behavioral deltas.
   - **`scores_just_imported` elimination**: Traced through the gate code to show it can be replaced by clearing `previous_plan_start_scores` sentinel in the import pre-step. One narrowing: partial trusted imports (documented, pinned with test).
   - **Canonical execution ownership (reigh bug)**: Plan owned work via overrides/clusters, but queue_order didn't list it, so phase logic thought work was drained. Fix: boundary detection must use `anchored_execution_items` (same set as `_phase_for_snapshot`).
   - **Ongoing**: User's last question ("is it clear what it's replacing? Will any functionality be lost?") was being addressed — I was reading the full doc and about to trace every piece of current functionality when the summary was requested.
